  -> create_file(content="// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Pipelined 16-lane posit(8,2) dot-product-accumulate unit.\n// Bit-exact re-implementation of the reference pdpu_top_pipelined arithmetic\n// with a compact datapath and 5 register stages (pipe1..pipe5).\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n`default_nettype none\n\nmodule pdpu_top_pipelined #(\n    parameter int unsigned N           = 16,\n    parameter int unsigned n_i         = 8,\n    parameter int unsigned es_i        = 2,\n    parameter int unsigned n_o         = 16,\n    parameter int unsigned es_o        = 2,\n    parameter int unsigned ALIGN_WIDTH = 14\n)(\n    input  logic             clk,\n    input  logic [N*n_i-1:0] operands_a,\n    input  logic [N*n_i-1:0] operands_b,\n    input  logic [n_o-1:0]   acc,\n    output logic [n_o-1:0]   result_o\n);\n\n  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n  // widths\n  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n  localparam int unsigned EW   = 7;             // exponent field (signed)\n  localparam int unsigned MI   = 4;             // input mantissa incl. hidden bit\n  localparam int unsigned MULW = 8;             // 4x4 product\n  localparam int unsigned MO   = 12;            // acc mantissa incl. hidden bit\n  localparam int unsigned ALW  = ALIGN_WIDTH;   // 14\n  localparam int unsigned SVW  = 15;            // signed aligned value width\n  localparam int unsigned SUML = 20;            // signed accumulator width\n  localparam int unsigned ML   = 19;            // magnitude width\n  localparam int unsigned RGW  = 15;            // regime field width\n\n  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n  // helper functions\n  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n  function automatic [3:0] lzc8 (input [7:0] v);\n    logic [3:0] c;\n    logic [7:0] t;\n    begin\n      c = 4'd0; t = v;\n      if (t[7:4] == 4'd0) begin c = c + 4'd4; t = t << 4; end\n      if (t[7:6] == 2'd0) begin c = c + 4'd2; t = t << 2; end\n      if (t[7]   == 1'b0) begin c = c + 4'd1; t = t << 1; end\n      if (t[7]   == 1'b0) c = c + 4'd1;\n      lzc8 = c;\n    end\n  endfunction\n\n  function automatic [4:0] lzc16 (input [15:0] v);\n    logic [4:0]  c;\n    logic [15:0] t;\n    begin\n      c = 5'd0; t = v;\n      if (t[15:8]  == 8'd0) begin c = c + 5'd8; t = t << 8; end\n      if (t[15:12] == 4'd0) begin c = c + 5'd4; t = t << 4; end\n      if (t[15:14] == 2'd0) begin c = c + 5'd2; t = t << 2; end\n      if (t[15]    == 1'b0) begin c = c + 5'd1; t = t << 1; end\n      if (t[15]    == 1'b0) c = c + 5'd1;\n      lzc16 = c;\n    end\n  endfunction\n\n  // leading-zero count of a 19-bit vector (0..19)\n  function automatic [4:0] lzc19 (input [18:0] v);\n    logic [4:0] c;\n    begin\n      if (v[18:11] == 8'd0) begin\n        if (v[10:3] == 8'd0) begin\n          if (v[2:1] == 2'd0) c = v[0] ? 5'd18 : 5'd19;\n          else                c = v[2] ? 5'd16 : 5'd17;\n        end else begin\n          if (v[10:7] == 4'd0) begin\n            if (v[6:5] == 2'd0) c = v[4] ? 5'd14 : 5'd15;\n            else                c = v[6] ? 5'd12 : 5'd13;\n          end else begin\n            if (v[10:9] == 2'd0) c = v[8]  ? 5'd10 : 5'd11;\n            else                 c = v[10] ? 5'd8  : 5'd9;\n          end\n        end\n      end else begin\n        if (v[18:15] == 4'd0) begin\n          if (v[14:13] == 2'd0) c = v[12] ? 5'd6 : 5'd7;\n          else                  c = v[14] ? 5'd4 : 5'd5;\n        end else begin\n          if (v[18:17] == 2'd0) c = v[16] ? 5'd2 : 5'd3;\n          else                  c = v[18] ? 5'd0 : 5'd1;\n        end\n      end\n      lzc19 = c;\n    end\n  endfunction\n\n  // 8-bit posit(8,2) decoder -> {sign, rg_exp[5:0], mant[3:0]}\n  function automatic [10:0] dec8 (input [7:0] p);\n    logic              sgn;\n    logic [6:0]        m, mv, lzop, op;\n    logic              regs, emp;\n    logic [3:0]        lz;\n    logic [2:0]        rl, rb;\n    logic signed [3:0] rk;\n    begin\n      sgn  = p[7];\n      m    = p[6:0];\n      mv   = sgn ? (7'd0 - m) : m;\n      regs = mv[6];\n      lzop = regs ? ~mv : mv;\n      emp  = (lzop == 7'd0);\n      lz   = lzc8({1'b0, lzop}) - 4'd1;\n      rl   = emp ? 3'd7 : lz[2:0];\n      rb   = emp ? 3'd7 : (lz[2:0] + 3'd1);\n      rk   = regs ? {1'b0, (rl - 3'd1)} : {1'b1, (~rl + 3'd1)};\n      op   = mv << rb;\n      dec8 = {sgn, ({{2{rk[3]}}, rk} <<< 2) | {4'b0, op[6:5]}, {|m, op[4:2]}};\n    end\n  endfunction\n\n  // 16-bit posit(16,2) decoder -> {sign, rg_exp[6:0], mant[11:0]}\n  function automatic [19:0] dec16 (input [15:0] p);\n    logic               sgn;\n    logic [14:0]        m, mv, lzop, op;\n    logic               regs, emp;\n    logic [4:0]         lz;\n    logic [3:0]         rl, rb;\n    logic signed [4:0]  rk;\n    begin\n      sgn  = p[15];\n      m    = p[14:0];\n      mv   = sgn ? 	operand_i,
	sign_o,
	rg_exp_o,
	mant_norm_o
);
	parameter [31:0] n = 16;
	parameter [31:0] es = 1;
	function automatic integer pdpu_pkg_clog2;
		input integer n;
		begin
			n = n - 1;
			for (pdpu_pkg_clog2 = 0; n > 0; pdpu_pkg_clog2 = pdpu_pkg_clog2 + 1)
				n = n >> 1;
		end
	endfunction
	parameter [31:0] nd = pdpu_pkg_clog2(n - 1);
	parameter [31:0] EXP_WIDTH = nd + es;
	parameter [31:0] MANT_WIDTH = (n - es) - 3;
	input wire [n - 1:0] operand_i;
	output wire sign_o;
	output wire signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] rg_exp_o;
	output wire [MANT_WIDTH:0] mant_norm_o;
	wire sign;

	assign sign = operand_i[n - 1];
	assign operand_value = (sign ? ~operand_i[n - 2:0] + 1 : operand_i[n - 2:0]);

I found the bug — off-by-one in the packed array widths (17 elements need `17*W` bits, not `16*W`). Let me rewrite carefully.




  -> create_file(content="// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Pipelined 16-lane posit(8,2) dot-product-accumulate unit.\n// Bit-exact re-implementation of the reference pdpu_top_pipelined arithmetic\n// with a compact datapath and 5 register stages (pipe1..pipe5).\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n`default_nettype none\n\nmodule pdpu_top_pipelined #(\n    parameter int unsigned N           = 16,\n    parameter int unsigned n_i         = 8,\n    parameter int unsigned es_i        = 2,\n    parameter int unsigned n_o         = 16,\n    parameter int unsigned es_o        = 2,\n    parameter int unsigned ALIGN_WIDTH = 14\n)(\n    input  logic             clk,\n    input  logic [N*n_i-1:0] operands_a,\n    input  logic [N*n_i-1:0] operands_b,\n    input  logic [n_o-1:0]   acc,\n    output logic [n_o-1:0]   result_o\n);\n\n  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n  // widths\n  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n  localparam int unsigned EW   = 7;             // exponent field (signed)\n  localparam int unsigned MI   = 4;             // input mantissa incl. hidden bit\n  localparam int unsigned MULW = 8;             // 4x4 product\n  localparam int unsigned MO   = 12;            // acc mantissa incl. hidden bit\n  localparam int unsigned ALW  = ALIGN_WIDTH;   // 14\n  localparam int unsigned SVW  = 15;            // signed aligned value width\n  localparam int unsigned SUML = 20;            // signed accumulator width\n  localparam int unsigned ML   = 19;            // magnitude width\n  localparam int unsigned NT   = N + 1;         // number of summands (17)\n\n  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n  // helper functions\n  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n  function automatic [3:0] lzc8 (input [7:0] v);\n    logic [3:0] c;\n    logic [7:0] t;\n    begin\n      c = 4'd0; t = v;\n      if (t[7:4] == 4'd0) begin c = c + 4'd4; t = t << 4; end\n      if (t[7:6] == 2'd0) begin c = c + 4'd2; t = t << 2; end\n      if (t[7]   == 1'b0) begin c = c + 4'd1; t = t << 1; end\n      if (t[7]   == 1'b0) c = c + 4'd1;\n      lzc8 = c;\n    end\n  endfunction\n\n  function automatic [4:0] lzc16 (input [15:0] v);\n    logic [4:0]  c;\n    logic [15:0] t;\n    begin\n      c = 5'd0; t = v;\n      if (t[15:8]  == 8'd0) begin c = c + 5'd8; t = t << 8; end\n      if (t[15:12] == 4'd0) begin c = c + 5'd4; t = t << 4; end\n      if (t[15:14] == 2'd0) begin c = c + 5'd2; t = t << 2; end\n      if (t[15]    == 1'b0) begin c = c + 5'd1; t = t << 1; end\n      if (t[15]    == 1'b0) c = c + 5'd1;\n      lzc16 = c;\n    end\n  endfunction\n\n  // leading-zero count of a 19-bit vector (0..19)\n  function automatic [4:0] lzc19 (input [18:0] v);\n    logic [4:0] c;\n    begin\n      if (v[18:11] == 8'd0) begin\n        if (v[10:3] == 8'd0) begin\n          if (v[2:1] == 2'd0) c = v[0] ? 5'd18 : 5'd19;\n          else                c = v[2] ? 5'd16 : 5'd17;\n        end else begin\n          if (v[10:7] == 4'd0) begin\n            if (v[6:5] == 2'd0) c = v[4] ? 5'd14 : 5'd15;\n            else                c = v[6] ? 5'd12 : 5'd13;\n          end else begin\n            if (v[10:9] == 2'd0) c = v[8]  ? 5'd10 : 5'd11;\n            else                 c = v[10] ? 5'd8  : 5'd9;\n          end\n        end\n      end else begin\n        if (v[18:15] == 4'd0) begin\n          if (v[14:13] == 2'd0) c = v[12] ? 5'd6 : 5'd7;\n          else                  c = v[14] ? 5'd4 : 5'd5;\n        end else begin\n          if (v[18:17] == 2'd0) c = v[16] ? 5'd2 : 5'd3;\n          else                  c = v[18] ? 5'd0 : 5'd1;\n        end\n      end\n      lzc19 = c;\n    end\n  endfunction\n\n  // 8-bit posit(8,2) decoder -> {sign, rg_exp[5:0], mant[3:0]}\n  function automatic [10:0] dec8 (input [7:0] p);\n    logic              sgn;\n    logic [6:0]        m, mv, lzop, op;\n    logic              regs, emp;\n    logic [3:0]        lz;\n    logic [2:0]        rl, rb;\n    logic signed [3:0] rk;\n    logic [5:0]        rge;\n    begin\n      sgn  = p[7];\n      m    = p[6:0];\n      mv   = sgn ? (7'd0 - m) : m;\n      regs = mv[6];\n      lzop = regs ? ~mv : mv;\n      emp  = (lzop == 7'd0);\n      lz   = lzc8({1'b0, lzop}) - 4'd1;\n      rl   = emp ? 3'd7 : lz[2:0];\n      rb   = emp ? 3'd7 : (lz[2:0] + 3'd1);\n      rk   = regs ? {1'b0, (rl - 3'd1)} : {1'b1, (~rl + 3'd1)};\n      op   = mv << rb;\n      rge  = {{2{rk[3]}}, rk} <<< 2;\n      dec8 = {sgn, (rge | {4'b0, op[6:5]}), {|m, op[4:2]}};\n    end\n  endfunction\n\n  // 16-bit posit(16,2) decoder -> {sign, rg_exp[6:0], mant[11:0]}\n  function automatic [19:0] dec16 (input [15:0] p);\n    logic               sgn;\n    logic [14:0]        m, mv, lzop, op;\n    logic               regs, emp;\n    logic [4:0]         lz;\n    logic [3:0]         rl, rb;\n    logic signed [4:0]  rk;\n    logic [6:0]         rge;\n    begin\n      sgn  = p[15];\n      m    = p[14:0];\n      mv   = sgn ? (15'd0 - m) : m;\n      regs = mv[14];\n      lzop = regs ? ~mv : mv;\n      emp  = (lzop == 15'd0);\n      lz   = lzc16({1'b0, lzop}) - 5'd1;\n      rl   = emp ? 4'd15 : lz[3:0];\n      rb   = emp ? 4'd15 : (lz[3:0] + 4'd1);\n      rk   = regs ? {1'b0, (rl - 4'd1)} : {1'b1, (~rl + 4'd1)};\n      op   = mv << rb;\n      rge  = {{2{rk[4]}}, rk} <<< 2;\n      dec16 = {sgn, (rge | {5'b0, op[14:13]}), {|m, op[12:2]}};\n    end\n  endfunction\n\n  function automatic signed [EW-1:0] smax (input signed [EW-1:0] x,\n                                           input signed [EW-1:0] y);\n    smax = (x > y) ? x : y;\n  endfunction\n\n  // =====================================================================\n  // Stage 1 : posit decode\n  // =====================================================================\n  wire [N-1:0]     sgn_ab;\n  wire [N*EW-1:0]  exp_c;\n  wire [N*MI-1:0]  mant_a, mant_b;\n\n  genvar i;\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < N; i++) begin : g_dec\n      wire [10:0] da, db;\n      assign da = dec8(operands_a[i*n_i +: n_i]);\n      assign db = dec8(operands_b[i*n_i +: n_i]);\n      assign sgn_ab[i]          = da[10] ^ db[10];\n      assign exp_c[i*EW +: EW]  = $signed(da[9:4]) + $signed(db[9:4]);\n      assign mant_a[i*MI +: MI] = da[3:0];\n      assign mant_b[i*MI +: MI] = db[3:0];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  wire [19:0] dacc;\n  assign dacc = dec16(acc);\n\n  // pipe1 registers\n  logic [N:0]      p1_sgn;\n  logic [N*EW-1:0] p1_exp_c;\n  logic [N*MI-1:0] p1_mant_a, p1_mant_b;\n  logic [EW-1:0]   p1_exp_acc;\n  logic [MO-1:0]   p1_mant_acc;\n\n  always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n    p1_sgn      <= {dacc[19], sgn_ab};\n    p1_exp_c    <= exp_c;\n    p1_mant_a   <= mant_a;\n    p1_mant_b   <= mant_b;\n    p1_exp_acc  <= dacc[18:12];\n    p1_mant_acc <= dacc[11:0];\n  end\n\n  // =====================================================================\n  // Stage 2 : mantissa multiply + exponent max tree\n  // =====================================================================\n  wire [N*MULW-1:0] prod;\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < N; i++) begin : g_mul\n      assign prod[i*MULW +: MULW] = p1_mant_a[i*MI +: MI] * p1_mant_b[i*MI +: MI];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  // 17 exponent items: 16 lane product exponents + accumulator exponent\n  wire signed [NT*EW-1:0] ei;\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < N; i++) begin : g_ei\n      assign ei[i*EW +: EW] = p1_exp_c[i*EW +: EW];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n  assign ei[N*EW +: EW] = p1_exp_acc;\n\n  // balanced max tree: 17 -> 9 -> 5 -> 3 -> 2 -> 1\n  wire signed [9*EW-1:0] l1;\n  wire signed [5*EW-1:0] l2;\n  wire signed [3*EW-1:0] l3;\n  wire signed [2*EW-1:0] l4;\n  wire signed [EW-1:0]   emax;\n\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < 8; i++) begin : g_m1\n      assign l1[i*EW +: EW] = smax(ei[(2*i)*EW +: EW], ei[(2*i+1)*EW +: EW]);\n    end\n  endgenerate\n  assign l1[8*EW +: EW] = ei[N*EW +: EW];\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < 4; i++) begin : g_m2\n      assign l2[i*EW +: EW] = smax(l1[(2*i)*EW +: EW], l1[(2*i+1)*EW +: EW]);\n    end\n  endgenerate\n  assign l2[4*EW +: EW] = l1[8*EW +: EW];\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < 2; i++) begin : g_m3\n      assign l3[i*EW +: EW] = smax(l2[(2*i)*EW +: EW], l2[(2*i+1)*EW +: EW]);\n    end\n  endgenerate\n  assign l3[2*EW +: EW] = l2[4*EW +: EW];\n  assign l4[0*EW +: EW] = smax(l3[0*EW +: EW], l3[1*EW +: EW]);\n  assign l4[1*EW +: EW] = l3[2*EW +: EW];\n  assign emax = smax(l4[0*EW +: EW], l4[1*EW +: EW]);\n\n  // pipe2 registers\n  logic [N:0]              p2_sgn;\n  logic signed [EW-1:0]    p2_emax;\n  logic [N*MULW-1:0]       p2_prod;\n  logic [MO-1:0]           p2_mant_acc;\n  logic signed [NT*EW-1:0] p2_ei;\n\n  always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n    p2_sgn      <= p1_sgn;\n    p2_emax     <= emax;\n    p2_prod     <= prod;\n    p2_mant_acc <= p1_mant_acc;\n    p2_ei       <= ei;\n  end\n\n  // =====================================================================\n  // Stage 3 : exponent difference + alignment shift + sign apply\n  // =====================================================================\n  wire signed [NT*SVW-1:0] sval;\n\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; (~sum_res[ML-1:0] + 1'b1)\n                                       :   sum_res[ML-1:0];\n\n  // pipe4 registers\n  logic signed [EW-1:0] p4_emax;\n  logic                 p4_sgn;\n  logic [ML-1:0]        p4_mag;\n\n  always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n    p4_emax <= p3_emax;\n    p4_sgn  <= sum_sgn;\n    p4_mag  <= mag;\n  end\n\n  // =====================================================================\n  // Stage 5 : normalization + posit encoder window\n  // =====================================================================\n  wire [4:0]        lz     = lzc19(p4_mag);\n  wire [ML-1:0]     norm   = p4_mag << lz;                 // 19 bits\n  wire [13:0]       fmant  = {norm[ML-1:6], |norm[5:0]};   // 14 bits\n  wire signed [7:0] fexp8  = $signed({{1{p4_emax[EW-1]}}, p4_emax})\n                           + 8'sd6 - $signed({3'b0, lz});\n  wire [6:0]        fexp   = fexp8[6:0];\n\n  // encoder regime computation (mirrors the reference posit_encoder)\n  wire              R           = fexp[6];\n  wire signed [4:0] regime_k    = fexp[6:2];\n  wire [4:0]        regime_bits = R ? ((~regime_k) + 5'd2) : (regime_k + 5'd2);\n  wire [4:0]        sa          = (regime_bits >= 5'd16) ? 5'd16 : regime_bits;\n\n  wire [14:0]       regime = R ? 15'd1 : ~(15'd1);\n  wire [29:0]       M      = {regime, fexp[1:0], fmant[12:0]};\n  wire [45:0]       Mx     = {M, 16'b0};\n  wire [45:0]       X      = Mx >> sa;\n  wire [14:0]       vbr    = X[30:16];\n  wire              rbit   = X[15];\n  wire              sticky = |X[14:0];\n  wire              rval   = rbit & (sticky | vbr[0]);\n  wire [14:0]       vaf    = vbr + {14'd0, rval};\n\n  // pipe5 registers\n  logic        p5_sgn, p5_nz;\n  logic [14:0] p5_vaf;\n\n  always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n    p5_sgn <= p4_sgn;\n    p5_nz  <= norm[ML-1];\n    p5_vaf <= vaf;\n  end\n\n  // =====================================================================\n  // output : sign + round finalize\n  // =====================================================================\n  wire [14:0] neg_val = (~p5_vaf) + 15'd1;\n  assign result_o = p5_nz ?   -> create_file(content="// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Pipelined 16-lane posit(8,2) dot-product-accumulate unit.\n// Bit-exact re-implementation of the reference pdpu_top_pipelined arithmetic\n// with a compact datapath and 5 register stages (pipe1..pipe5).\n//\n// Key optimizations vs. the reference:\n//  * decoded exponent field kept at 7 bits (max range +-64), mantissas reduced\n//    to the bits that actually reach the output;\n//  * sign application moved out of the align stage: |aligned| XOR sign plus a\n//    popcount correction folded into the accumulator (removes 17 conditional\n//    two's-complement negations);\n//  * two-level saturating shift-amount clamp;\n//  * the posit encoder's variable right shift is expressed as a window read of\n//    a structured vector instead of a 46-bit barrel shifter.\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n`default_nettype none\n\nmodule pdpu_top_pipelined #(\n    parameter int unsigned N           = 16,\n    parameter int unsigned n_i         = 8,\n    parameter int unsigned es_i        = 2,\n    parameter int unsigned n_o         = 16,\n    parameter int unsigned es_o        = 2,\n    parameter int unsigned ALIGN_WIDTH = 14\n)(\n    input  logic             clk,\n    input  logic [N*n_i-1:0] operands_a,\n    input  logic [N*n_i-1:0] operands_b,\n    input  logic [n_o-1:0]   acc,\n    output logic [n_o-1:0]   result_o\n);\n\n  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n  // widths\n  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n  localparam int unsigned EW   = 7;             // exponent field (signed)\n  localparam int unsigned MI   = 4;             // input mantissa incl. hidden bit\n  localparam int unsigned MULW = 8;             // 4x4 product\n  localparam int unsigned MO   = 12;            // acc mantissa incl. hidden bit\n  localparam int unsigned AW   = ALIGN_WIDTH;   // 14\n  localparam int unsigned ML   = 19;            // magnitude width\n  localparam int unsigned NT   = N + 1;         // number of summands (17)\n\n  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n  // helper functions\n  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n  function automatic [3:0] lzc8 (input [7:0] v);\n    logic [3:0] c;\n    logic [7:0] t;\n    begin\n      c = 4'd0; t = v;\n      if (t[7:4] == 4'd0) begin c = c + 4'd4; t = t << 4; end\n      if (t[7:6] == 2'd0) begin c = c + 4'd2; t = t << 2; end\n      if (t[7]   == 1'b0) begin c = c + 4'd1; t = t << 1; end\n      if (t[7]   == 1'b0) c = c + 4'd1;\n      lzc8 = c;\n    end\n  endfunction\n\n  function automatic [4:0] lzc16 (input [15:0] v);\n    logic [4:0]  c;\n    logic [15:0] t;\n    begin\n      c = 5'd0; t = v;\n      if (t[15:8]  == 8'd0) begin c = c + 5'd8; t = t << 8; end\n      if (t[15:12] == 4'd0) begin c = c + 5'd4; t = t << 4; end\n      if (t[15:14] == 2'd0) begin c = c + 5'd2; t = t << 2; end\n      if (t[15]    == 1'b0) begin c = c + 5'd1; t = t << 1; end\n      if (t[15]    == 1'b0) c = c + 5'd1;\n      lzc16 = c;\n    end\n  endfunction\n\n  // leading-zero count of a 19-bit vector (0..19)\n  function automatic [4:0] lzc19 (input [18:0] v);\n    logic [4:0] c;\n    begin\n      if (v[18:11] == 8'd0) begin\n        if (v[10:3] == 8'd0) begin\n          if (v[2:1] == 2'd0) c = v[0] ? 5'd18 : 5'd19;\n          else                c = v[2] ? 5'd16 : 5'd17;\n        end else begin\n          if (v[10:7] == 4'd0) begin\n            if (v[6:5] == 2'd0) c = v[4] ? 5'd14 : 5'd15;\n            else                c = v[6] ? 5'd12 : 5'd13;\n          end else begin\n            if (v[10:9] == 2'd0) c = v[8]  ? 5'd10 : 5'd11;\n            else                 c = v[10] ? 5'd8  : 5'd9;\n          end\n        end\n      end else begin\n        if (v[18:15] == 4'd0) begin\n          if (v[14:13] == 2'd0) c = v[12] ? 5'd6 : 5'd7;\n          else                  c = v[14] ? 5'd4 : 5'd5;\n        end else begin\n          if (v[18:17] == 2'd0) c = v[16] ? 5'd2 : 5'd3;\n          else                  c = v[18] ? 5'd0 : 5'd1;\n        end\n      end\n      lzc19 = c;\n    end\n  endfunction\n\n  // 8-bit posit(8,2) decoder -> {sign, rg_exp[5:0], mant[3:0]}\n  function automatic [10:0] dec8 (input [7:0] p);\n    logic              sgn;\n    logic [6:0]        m, mv, lzop, op;\n    logic              regs, emp;\n    logic [3:0]        lz;\n    logic [2:0]        rl, rb;\n    logic signed [3:0] rk;\n    logic [5:0]        rge;\n    begin\n      sgn  = p[7];\n      m    = p[6:0];\n      mv   = sgn ? (7'd0 - m) : m;\n      regs = mv[6];\n      lzop = regs ? ~mv : mv;\n      emp  = (lzop == 7'd0);\n      lz   = lzc8({1'b0, lzop}) - 4'd1;\n      rl   = emp ? 3'd7 : lz[2:0];\n      rb   = emp ? 3'd7 : (lz[2:0] + 3'd1);\n      rk   = regs ? {1'b0, (rl - 3'd1)} : {1'b1, (~rl + 3'd1)};\n      op   = mv << rb;\n      rge  = {{2{rk[3]}}, rk} <<< 2;\n      dec8 = {sgn, (rge | {4'b0, op[6:5]}), {|m, op[4:2]}};\n    end\n  endfunction\n\n  // 16-bit posit(16,2) decoder -> {sign, rg_exp[6:0], mant[11:0]}\n  function automatic [19:0] dec16 (input [15:0] p);\n    logic               sgn;\n    logic [14:0]        m, mv, lzop, op;\n    logic               regs, emp;\n    logic [4:0]         lz;\n    logic [3:0]         rl, rb;\n    logic signed [4:0]  rk;\n    logic [6:0]         rge;\n    begin\n      sgn  = p[15];\n      m    = p[14:0];\n      mv   = sgn ? (15'd0 - m) : m;\n      regs = mv[14];\n      lzop = regs ? ~mv : mv;\n      emp  = (lzop == 15'd0);\n      lz   = lzc16({1'b0, lzop}) - 5'd1;\n      rl   = emp ? 4'd15 : lz[3:0];\n      rb   = emp ? 4'd15 : (lz[3:0] + 4'd1);\n      rk   = regs ? {1'b0, (rl - 4'd1)} : {1'b1, (~rl + 4'd1)};\n      op   = mv << rb;\n      rge  = {{2{rk[4]}}, rk} <<< 2;\n      dec16 = {sgn, (rge | {5'b0, op[14:13]}), {|m, op[12:2]}};\n    end\n  endfunction\n\n  function automatic signed [EW-1:0] smax (input signed [EW-1:0] x,\n                                           input signed [EW-1:0] y);\n    smax = (x > y) ? x : y;\n  endfunction\n\n  // =====================================================================\n  // Stage 1 : posit decode\n  // =====================================================================\n  wire [N-1:0]     sgn_ab;\n  wire [N*EW-1:0]  exp_c;\n  wire [N*MI-1:0]  mant_a, mant_b;\n\n  genvar i;\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < N; i++) begin : g_dec\n      wire [10:0] da, db;\n      assign da = dec8(operands_a[i*n_i +: n_i]);\n      assign db = dec8(operands_b[i*n_i +: n_i]);\n      assign sgn_ab[i]          = da[10] ^ db[10];\n      assign exp_c[i*EW +: EW]  = $signed(da[9:4]) + $signed(db[9:4]);\n      assign mant_a[i*MI +: MI] = da[3:0];\n      assign mant_b[i*MI +: MI] = db[3:0];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  wire [19:0] dacc;\n  assign dacc = dec16(acc);\n\n  // pipe1 registers\n  logic [N:0]      p1_sgn;\n  logic [N*EW-1:0] p1_exp_c;\n  logic [N*MI-1:0] p1_mant_a, p1_mant_b;\n  logic [EW-1:0]   p1_exp_acc;\n  logic [MO-1:0]   p1_mant_acc;\n\n  always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n    p1_sgn      <= {dacc[19], sgn_ab};\n    p1_exp_c    <= exp_c;\n    p1_mant_a   <= mant_a;\n    p1_mant_b   <= mant_b;\n    p1_exp_acc  <= dacc[18:12];\n    p1_mant_acc <= dacc[11:0];\n  end\n\n  // =====================================================================\n  // Stage 2 : mantissa multiply + exponent max tree\n  // =====================================================================\n  wire [N*MULW-1:0] prod;\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < N; i++) begin : g_mul\n      assign prod[i*MULW +: MULW] = p1_mant_a[i*MI +: MI] * p1_mant_b[i*MI +: MI];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  wire signed [NT*EW-1:0] ei;\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < N; i++) begin : g_ei\n      assign ei[i*EW +: EW] = p1_exp_c[i*EW +: EW];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n  assign ei[N*EW +: EW] = p1_exp_acc;\n\n  // balanced max tree: 17 -> 9 -> 5 -> 3 -> 2 -> 1\n  wire signed [9*EW-1:0] l1;\n  wire signed [5*EW-1:0] l2;\n  wire signed [3*EW-1:0] l3;\n  wire signed [2*EW-1:0] l4;\n  wire signed [EW-1:0]   emax;\n\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < 8; i++) begin : g_m1\n      assign l1[i*EW +: EW] = smax(ei[(2*i)*EW +: EW], ei[(2*i+1)*EW +: EW]);\n    end\n  endgenerate\n  assign l1[8*EW +: EW] = ei[N*EW +: EW];\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < 4; i++) begin : g_m2\n      assign l2[i*EW +: EW] = smax(l1[(2*i)*EW +: EW], l1[(2*i+1)*EW +: EW]);\n    end\n  endgenerate\n  assign l2[4*EW +: EW] = l1[8*EW +: EW];\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < 2; i++) begin : g_m3\n      assign l3[i*EW +: EW] = smax(l2[(2*i)*EW +: EW], l2[(2*i+1)*EW +: EW]);\n    end\n  endgenerate\n  assign l3[2*EW +: EW] = l2[4*EW +: EW];\n  assign l4[0*EW +: EW] = smax(l3[0*EW +: EW], l3[1*EW +: EW]);\n  assign l4[1*EW +: EW] = l3[2*EW +: EW];\n  assign emax = smax(l4[0*EW +: EW], l4[1*EW +: EW]);\n\n  // pipe2 registers\n  logic [N:0]              p2_sgn;\n  logic signed [EW-1:0]    p2_emax;\n  logic [N*MULW-1:0]       p2_prod;\n  logic [MO-1:0]           p2_mant_acc;\n  logic signed [NT*EW-1:0] p2_ei;\n\n  always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n    p2_sgn      <= p1_sgn;\n    p2_emax     <= emax;\n    p2_prod     <= prod;\n    p2_mant_acc <= p1_mant_acc;\n    p2_ei       <= ei;\n  end\n\n  // =====================================================================\n  // Stage 3 : exponent difference + alignment shift\n  // =====================================================================\n  wire [NT*AW-1:0] aval;   // magnitude of the aligned value (unsigned)\n\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < NT; i++) begin : g_align\n      wire [EW-1:0]  diff;\n      wire [3:0]     sh;\n      wire [AW-1:0]  pfull;\n      assign diff = $unsigned(p2_emax - $signed(p2_ei[i*EW +: EW]));\n      // saturating clamp: values >= 14 all shift the payload out\n      assign sh[3:2] = (&diff[EW-1:4]) ? 2'b11 : diff[3:2];\n      assign sh[1:0] = diff[1:0];\n      if (i < N) begin : g_lane\n        assign pfull = {p2_prod[i*MULW +: MULW], 6'b0};\n      end\n      else begin : g_acc\n        assign pfull = {1'b0, p2_mant_acc, 1'b0};\n      end\n      assign aval[i*AW +: AW] = pfull >> sh;\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  // pipe3 registers\n  logic signed [EW-1:0] p3_emax;\n  logic [NT*AW-1:0]     p3_aval;\n  logic [N:0]           p3_sgn;\n\n  always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n    p3_emax <= p2_emax;\n    p3_aval <= aval;\n    p3_sgn  <= p2_sgn;\n  end\n\n  // =====================================================================\n  // Stage 4 : two's-complement accumulation\n  //   sum = SUM_i (a_i XOR s_i) + popcount(s) * 2**AW\n  // =====================================================================\n  wire [NT*AW-1:0] p3_mask;\n  wire [N:0]       p3_neg = p3_sgn;\n\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < NT; i++) begin : g_mask\n      assign p3_mask[i*AW +: AW] = {AW{p3_neg[i]}};\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  wire [NT*AW-1:0] mval;\n  assign mval = p3_aval ^ p3_mask;\n\n  // popcount of the 17 sign bits (5 bits)\n  wire [1:0] pp [0:8];\n  wire [2:0] pq [0:4];\n  wire [3:0] pr [0:2];\n  wire [4:0] pop;\n\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < 8; i++) begin : g_pp\n      assign pp[i] = {1'b0, p3_neg[2*i]} + {1'b0, p3_neg[2*i+1]};\n    end\n  endgenerate\n  assign pp[8] = {1'b0, p3_neg[16]};\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < 4; i++) begin : g_pq\n      assign pq[i] = {1'b0, pp[2*i]} + {1'b0, pp[2*i+1]};\n    end\n  endgenerate\n  assign pq[4] = {1'b0, pp[8]};\n  assign pr[0] = {1'b0, pq[0]} + {1'b0, pq[1]};\n  assign pr[1] = {1'b0, pq[2]} + {1'b0, pq[3]};\n  assign pr[2] = {1'b0, pq[4]};\n  assign pop = ({1'b0, pr[0]} + {1'b0, pr[1]}) + {1'b0, pr[2]};\n\n  // 17 -> 9 -> 5 -> 3 -> 2 -> 1 adder tree with tight widths\n  wire [14:0] s1 [0:8];\n  wire [15:0] s2 [0:4];\n  wire [16:0] s3 [0:2];\n  wire [17:0] s4 [0:1];\n  wire [18:0] s5;\n\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < 8; i++) begin : g_s1\n      assign s1[i] = mval[(2*i)*AW +: AW] + mval[(2*i+1)*AW +: AW];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n  assign s1[8] = {1'b0, mval[16*AW +: AW]};\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < 4; i++) begin : g_s2\n      assign s2[i] = s1[2*i] + s1[2*i+1];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n  assign s2[4] = {1'b0, s1[8]};\n  assign s3[0] = s2[0] + s2[1];\n  assign s3[1] = s2[2] + s2[3];\n  assign s3[2] = {1'b0, s2[4]};\n  assign s4[0] = s3[0] + s3[1];\n  assign s4[1] = {1'b0, s3[2]};\n  assign s5 = s4[0] + s4[1];\n\n  // add pop * 2**AW : only bits >= AW are affected\n  wire [ML-1:0] total;\n  wire [5:0]    hi;\n  assign hi    = {1'b0, s5[18:AW]} + {1'b0, pop};\n  assign total = {hi, s5[AW-1:0]};\n\n  wire          sum_sgn = total[ML-1];\n  wire [ML-1:0] mag     = sum_sgn ? (~total + 1'b1) : total;\n\n  // pipe4 registers\n  logic signed [EW-1:0] p4_emax;\n  logic                 p4_sgn;\n  logic [ML-1:0]        p4_mag;\n\n  always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n    p4_emax <= p3_emax;\n    p4_sgn  <= sum_sgn;\n    p4_mag  <= mag;\n  end\n\n  // =====================================================================\n  // Stage 5 : normalization + posit encoder\n  // =====================================================================\n  wire [4:0]        lz     = lzc19(p4_mag);\n  wire [ML-1:0]     norm   = p4_mag << lz;                 // 19 bits\n  wire [13:0]       fmant  = {norm[ML-1:6], |norm[5:0]};   // 14 bits\n  wire signed [7:0] fexp8  = $signed({{1{p4_emax[EW-1]}}, p4_emax})\n                           + 8'sd6 - $signed({3'b0, lz});\n  wire [6:0]        fexp   = fexp8[6:0];\n\n  wire              R           = fexp[6];\n  wire signed [4:0] regime_k    = fexp[6:2];\n  wire [4:0]        regime_bits = R ? ((~regime_k) + 5'd2) : (regime_k + 5'd2);\n  wire [4:0]        sa          = (regime_bits >= 5'd16) ? 5'd16 : regime_bits;\n\n  // fv = {exp, fmant}: the part of rg_exp_mant below the (constant) regime run\n  wire [14:0] fv  = {fexp[1:0], fmant[12:0]};\n  // 32-bit structured vector: bits 31:16 = R, 15:1 = fv, bit 0 = 0\n  wire [31:0] Z   = {{16{R}}, fv, 1'b0};\n  wire [15:0] Y   = (Z >> sa);\n  // Y[15] must be 0 when sa == 16\n  wire [15:0] Yc  = {Y[15] & ~(sa[4] & R), Y[14:0]};\n  wire        rbit = Yc[0];\n  wire [14:0] vbr  = Yc[15:1];\n  wire        sticky = |(Z & ((32'd1 << sa) - 32'd1));\n  wire        rval = rbit & (sticky | vbr[0]);\n  wire [14:0] vaf  = vbr + {14'd0, rval};\n\n  // pipe5 registers\n  logic        p5_sgn, p5_nz;\n  logic [14:0] p5_vaf;\n\n  always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n    p5_sgn <= p4_sgn;\n    p5_nz  <= norm[ML-1];\n    p5_vaf <= vaf;\n  end\n\n  wire [14:0] neg_val = (~p5_vaf) + 15'd1;\n  assign result_o = p5_nz ? (p5_sgn ? {1'b1, neg_val} : {1'b0, p5_vaf})\n                          : {n_o{1'b0}};\n\nendmodule\n\n`default_nettype wire\n", filename='design2.sv')

t = v;\n      if (t[7:4] == 4'd0) begin c = c + 4'd4; t = t << 4; end\n      if (t[7:6] == 2'd0) begin c = c + 4'd2; t = t << 2; end\n      if (t[7]   == 1'b0) begin c = c + 4'd1; t = t << 1; end\n      if (t[7]   == 1'b0) c = c + 4'd1;\n      lzc8 = c;\n    end\n  endfunction\n\n  function automatic [4:0] lzc16 (input [15:0] v);\n    logic [4:0]  c;\n    logic [15:0] t;\n    begin\n      c = 5'd0; t = v;\n      if (t[15:8]  == 8'd0) begin c = c + 5'd8; t = t << 8; end\n      if (t[15:12] == 4'd0) begin c = c + 5'd4; t = t << 4; end\n      if (t[15:14] == 2'd0) begin c = c + 5'd2; t = t << 2; end\n      if (t[15]    == 1'b0) begin c = c + 5'd1; t = t << 1; end\n      if (t[15]    == 1'b0) c = c + 5'd1;\n      lzc16 = c;\n    end\n  endfunction\n\n  function automatic [4:0] lzc19 (input [18:0] v);\n    logic [4:0] c;\n    begin\n      if (v[18:11] == 8'd0) begin\n        if (v[10:3] == 8'd0) begin\n          if (v[2:1] == 2'd0) c = v[0] ? 5'd18 : 5'd19;\n          else                c = v[2] ? 5'd16 : 5'd17;\n        end else begin\n          if (v[10:7] == 4'd0) begin\n            if (v[6:5] == 2'd0) c = v[4] ? 5'd14 : 5'd15;\n            else                c = v[6] ? 5'd12 : 5'd13;\n          end else begin\n            if (v[10:9] == 2'd0) c = v[8]  ? 5'd10 : 5'd11;\n            else                 c = v[10] ? 5'd8  : 5'd9;\n          end\n        end\n      end else begin\n        if (v[18:15] == 4'd0) begin\n          if (v[14:13] == 2'd0) c = v[12] ? 5'd6 : 5'd7;\n          else                  c = v[14] ? 5'd4 : 5'd5;\n        end else begin\n          if (v[18:17] == 2'd0) c = v[16] ? 5'd2 : 5'd3;\n          else                  c = v[18] ? 5'd0 : 5'd1;\n        end\n      end\n      lzc19 = c;\n    end\n  endfunction\n\n  function automatic [10:0] dec8 (input [7:0] p);\n    logic              sgn;\n    logic [6:0]        m, mv, lzop, op;\n    logic              regs, emp;\n    logic [3:0]        lz;\n    logic [2:0]        rl, rb;\n    logic signed [3:0] rk;\n    logic [5:0]        rge;\n    begin\n      sgn  = p[7];\n      m    = p[6:0];\n      mv   = sgn ? (7'd0 - m) : m;\n      regs = mv[6];\n      lzop = regs ? ~mv : mv;\n      emp  = (lzop == 7'd0);\n      lz   = lzc8({1'b0, lzop}) - 4'd1;\n      rl   = emp ? 3'd7 : lz[2:0];\n      rb   = emp ? 3'd7 : (lz[2:0] + 3'd1);\n      rk   = regs ? {1'b0, (rl - 3'd1)} : {1'b1, (~rl + 3'd1)};\n      op   = mv << rb;\n      rge  = {{2{rk[3]}}, rk} <<< 2;\n      dec8 = {sgn, (rge | {4'b0, op[6:5]}), {|m, op[4:2]}};\n    end\n  endfunction\n\n  function automatic [19:0] dec16 (input [15:0] p);\n    logic               sgn;\n    logic [14:0]        m, mv, lzop, op;\n    logic               regs, emp;\n    logic [4:0]         lz;\n    logic [3:0]         rl, rb;\n    logic signed [4:0]  rk;\n    logic [6:0]         rge;\n    begin\n      sgn  = p[15];\n      m    = p[14:0];\n      mv   = sgn ? (15'd0 - m) : m;\n      regs = mv[14];\n      lzop = regs ? ~mv : mv;\n      emp  = (lzop == 15'd0);\n      lz   = lzc16({1'b0, lzop}) - 5'd1;\n      rl   = emp ? 4'd15 : lz[3:0];\n      rb   = emp ? 4'd15 : (lz[3:0] + 4'd1);\n      rk   = regs ? {1'b0, (rl - 4'd1)} : {1'b1, (~rl + 4'd1)};\n      op   = mv << rb;\n      rge  = {{2{rk[4]}}, rk} <<< 2;\n      dec16 = {sgn, (rge | {5'b0, op[14:13]}), {|m, op[12:2]}};\n    end\n  endfunction\n\n  function automatic signed [EW-1:0] smax (input signed [EW-1:0] x,\n                                           input signed [EW-1:0] y);\n    smax = (x > y) ? x : y;\n  endfunction\n\n  // =====================================================================\n  // Stage 1 : posit decode\n  // =====================================================================\n  wire [N-1:0]     sgn_ab;\n  wire [N*EW-1:0]  exp_c;\n  wire [N*MI-1:0]  mant_a, mant_b;\n\n  genvar i;\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < N; i++) begin : g_dec\n      wire [10:0] da, db;\n      assign da = dec8(operands_a[i*n_i +: n_i]);\n      assign db = dec8(operands_b[i*n_i +: n_i]);\n      assign sgn_ab[i]          = da[10] ^ db[10];\n      assign exp_c[i*EW +: EW]  = $signed(da[9:4]) + $signed(db[9:4]);\n      assign mant_a[i*MI +: MI] = da[3:0];\n      assign mant_b[i*MI +: MI] = db[3:0];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  wire [19:0] dacc;\n  assign dacc = dec16(acc);\n\n  logic [N:0]      p1_sgn;\n  logic [N*EW-1:0] p1_exp_c;\n  logic [N*MI-1:0] p1_mant_a, p1_mant_b;\n  logic [EW-1:0]   p1_exp_acc;\n  logic [MO-1:0]   p1_mant_acc;\n\n  always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n    p1_sgn      <= {dacc[19], sgn_ab};\n    p1_exp_c    <= exp_c;\n    p1_mant_a   <= mant_a;\n    p1_mant_b   <= mant_b;\n    p1_exp_acc  <= dacc[18:12];\n    p1_mant_acc <= dacc[11:0];\n  end\n\n  // =====================================================================\n  // Stage 2 : mantissa multiply + exponent max tree\n  // =====================================================================\n  wire [N*MULW-1:0] prod;\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < N; i++) begin : g_mul\n      assign prod[i*MULW +: MULW] = p1_mant_a[i*MI +: MI] * p1_mant_b[i*MI +: MI];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  wire signed [NT*EW-1:0] ei;\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < N; i++) begin : g_ei\n      assign ei[i*EW +: EW] = p1_exp_c[i*EW +: EW];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n  assign ei[N*EW +: EW] = p1_exp_acc;\n\n  wire signed [9*EW-1:0] l1;\n  wire signed [5*EW-1:0] l2;\n  wire signed [3*EW-1:0] l3;\n  wire signed [2*EW-1:0] l4;\n  wire signed [EW-1:0]   emax;\n\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < 8; i++) begin : g_m1\n      assign l1[i*EW +: EW] = smax(ei[(2*i)*EW +: EW], ei[(2*i+1)*EW +: EW]);\n    end\n  endgenerate\n  assign l1[8*EW +: EW] = ei[N*EW +: EW];\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < 4; i++) begin : g_m2\n      assign l2[i*EW +: EW] = smax(l1[(2*i)*EW +: EW], l1[(2*i+1)*EW +: EW]);\n    end\n  endgenerate\n  assign l2[4*EW +: EW] = l1[8*EW +: EW];\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < 2; i++) begin : g_m3\n      assign l3[i*EW +: EW] = smax(l2[(2*i)*EW +: EW], l2[(2*i+1)*EW +: EW]);\n    end\n  endgenerate\n  assign l3[2*EW +: EW] = l2[4*EW +: EW];\n  assign l4[0*EW +: EW] = smax(l3[0*EW +: EW], l3[1*EW +: EW]);\n  assign l4[1*EW +: EW] = l3[2*EW +: EW];\n  assign emax = smax(l4[0*EW +: EW], l4[1*EW +: EW]);\n\n  logic [N:0]              p2_sgn;\n  logic signed [EW-1:0]    p2_emax;\n  logic [N*MULW-1:0]       p2_prod;\n  logic [MO-1:0]           p2_mant_acc;\n  logic signed [NT*EW-1:0] p2_ei;\n\n  always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n    p2_sgn      <= p1_sgn;\n    p2_emax     <= emax;\n    p2_prod     <= prod;\n    p2_mant_acc <= p1_mant_acc;\n    p2_ei       <= ei;\n  end\n\n  // =====================================================================\n  // Stage 3 : exponent difference + alignment shift (magnitude only)\n  // =====================================================================\n  wire [NT*AW-1:0] aval;\n\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < NT; i++) begin : g_align\n      wire signed [7:0] diff;\n      wire [3:0]        sh;\n      wire [AW-1:0]     pfull;\n      assign diff = {p2_emax[EW-1], p2_emax} - {p2_ei[i*EW+EW-1], p2_ei[i*EW +: EW]};\n      assign sh   = (|diff[7:4]) ? 4'd14 : diff[3:0];\n      if (i < N) begin : g_lane\n        assign pfull = {p2_prod[i*MULW +: MULW], 6'b0};\n      end\n      else begin : g_acc\n        assign pfull = {1'b0, p2_mant_acc, 1'b0};\n      end\n      assign aval[i*AW +: AW] = pfull >> sh;\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  logic signed [EW-1:0] p3_emax;\n  logic [NT*AW-1:0]     p3_aval;\n  logic [N:0]           p3_neg;\n\n  always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n    p3_emax <= p2_emax;\n    p3_aval <= aval;\n    p3_neg  <= p2_sgn;\n  end\n\n  // =====================================================================\n  // Stage 4 : two's-complement accumulation\n  //   sum = SUM_i (a_i XOR s_i) + popcount(s)\n  // =====================================================================\n  wire [NT*AW-1:0] mval;\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < NT; i++) begin : g_mask\n      assign mval[i*AW +: AW] = p3_aval[i*AW +: AW] ^ {AW{p3_neg[i]}};\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  wire [1:0] pp [0:8];\n  wire [2:0] pq [0:4];\n  wire [3:0] pr [0:2];\n  wire [4:0] pop;\n\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < 8; i++) begin : g_pp\n      assign pp[i] = {1'b0, p3_neg[2*i]} + {1'b0, p3_neg[2*i+1]};\n    end\n  endgenerate\n  assign pp[8] = {1'b0, p3_neg[16]};\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < 4; i++) begin : g_pq\n      assign pq[i] = {1'b0, pp[2*i]} + {1'b0, pp[2*i+1]};\n    end\n  endgenerate\n  assign pq[4] = {1'b0, pp[8]};\n  assign pr[0] = {1'b0, pq[0]} + {1'b0, pq[1]};\n  assign pr[1] = {1'b0, pq[2]} + {1'b0, pq[3]};\n  assign pr[2] = {1'b0, pq[4]};\n  assign pop = ({1'b0, pr[0]} + {1'b0, pr[1]}) + {1'b0, pr[2]};\n\n  wire [14:0] s1 [0:8];\n  wire [15:0] s2 [0:4];\n  wire [16:0] s3 [0:2];\n  wire [17:0] s4 [0:1];\n  wire [18:0] s5;\n\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < 8; i++) begin : g_s1\n      assign s1[i] = mval[(2*i)*AW +: AW] + mval[(2*i+1)*AW +: AW];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n  assign s1[8] = {1'b0, mval[16*AW +: AW]};\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < 4; i++) begin : g_s2\n      assign s2[i] = s1[2*i] + s1[2*i+1];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n  assign s2[4] = {1'b0, s1[8]};\n  assign s3[0] = s2[0] + s2[1];\n  assign s3[1] = s2[2] + s2[3];\n  assign s3[2] = {1'b0, s2[4]};\n  assign s4[0] = s3[0] + s3[1];\n  assign s4[1] = {1'b0, s3[2]};\n  assign s5 = s4[0] + s4[1];\n\n  wire [ML-1:0] total = s5 + {14'd0, pop};\n  wire          sum_sgn = total[ML-1];\n  wire [ML-1:0] mag     = sum_sgn ? (~total + 1'b1) : total;\n\n  logic signed [EW-1:0] p4_emax;\n  logic                 p4_sgn;\n  logic [ML-1:0]        p4_mag;\n\n  always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n    p4_emax <= p3_emax;\n    p4_sgn  <= sum_sgn;\n    p4_mag  <= mag;\n  end\n\n  // =====================================================================\n  // Stage 5 : normalization + posit encoder\n  // =====================================================================\n  wire [4:0]        lz     = lzc19(p4_mag);\n  wire [ML-1:0]     norm   = p4_mag << lz;\n  wire [12:0]       fmant  = {norm[ML-1:6], |norm[5:0]};\n  wire signed [7:0] fexp8  = $signed({{1{p4_emax[EW-1]}}, p4_emax})\n                           + 8'sd6 - $signed({3'b0, lz});\n  wire [6:0]        fexp   = fexp8[6:0];\n\n  wire              R           = fexp[6];\n  wire signed [4:0] regime_k    = fexp[6:2];\n  wire [4:0]        regime_bits = R ?   -> create_file(content="// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Pipelined 16-lane posit(8,2) dot-product-accumulate unit.\n// Bit-exact re-implementation of the reference pdpu_top_pipelined arithmetic\n// with a compact datapath and 5 register stages (pipe1..pipe5).\n//\n// Optimizations vs. the reference\n//  * decoded exponent field kept at 7 bits, mantissas reduced to the bits that\n//    actually reach the output (4-bit input mantissas, 8x8 lane product);\n//  * sign application moved out of the align stage: |aligned| XOR sign plus a\n//    popcount correction folded into the accumulator tree (removes 17\n//    conditional two's-complement negations and the sign-extension fan-out);\n//  * saturating clamp of the align shift amount;\n//  * the posit encoder's variable right shift is expressed as a single\n//    variable-position window read of a 31-bit vector.\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n`default_nettype none\n\nmodule pdpu_top_pipelined #(\n    parameter int unsigned N           = 16,\n    parameter int unsigned n_i         = 8,\n    parameter int unsigned es_i        = 2,\n    parameter int unsigned n_o         = 16,\n    parameter int unsigned es_o        = 2,\n    parameter int unsigned ALIGN_WIDTH = 14\n)(\n    input  logic             clk,\n    input  logic [N*n_i-1:0] operands_a,\n    input  logic [N*n_i-1:0] operands_b,\n    input  logic [n_o-1:0]   acc,\n    output logic [n_o-1:0]   result_o\n);\n\n  localparam int unsigned EW   = 7;             // signed exponent field\n  localparam int unsigned MI   = 4;             // input mantissa incl. hidden bit\n  localparam int unsigned MULW = 8;             // 4x4 lane product\n  localparam int unsigned MO   = 12;            // acc mantissa incl. hidden bit\n  localparam int unsigned AW   = ALIGN_WIDTH;   // 14\n  localparam int unsigned ML   = 19;            // result magnitude width\n  localparam int unsigned NT   = N + 1;         // 17 summands\n\n  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n  // helper functions\n  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n  function automatic [3:0] lzc8 (input [7:0] v);\n    logic [3:0] c;\n    logic [7:0] t;\n    begin\n      c = 4'd0; t = v;\n      if (t[7:4] == 4'd0) begin c = c + 4'd4; t = t << 4; end\n      if (t[7:6] == 2'd0) begin c = c + 4'd2; t = t << 2; end\n      if (t[7]   == 1'b0) begin c = c + 4'd1; t = t << 1; end\n      if (t[7]   == 1'b0) c = c + 4'd1;\n      lzc8 = c;\n    end\n  endfunction\n\n  function automatic [4:0] lzc16 (input [15:0] v);\n    logic [4:0]  c;\n    logic [15:0] t;\n    begin\n      c = 5'd0; t = v;\n      if (t[15:8]  == 8'd0) begin c = c + 5'd8; t = t << 8; end\n      if (t[15:12] == 4'd0) begin c = c + 5'd4; t = t << 4; end\n      if (t[15:14] == 2'd0) begin c = c + 5'd2; t = t << 2; end\n      if (t[15]    == 1'b0) begin c = c + 5'd1; t = t << 1; end\n      if (t[15]    == 1'b0) c = c + 5'd1;\n      lzc16 = c;\n    end\n  endfunction\n\n  // leading-zero count of a 19-bit vector (0..19)\n  function automatic [4:0] lzc19 (input [18:0] v);\n    logic [4:0] c;\n    begin\n      if (v[18:11] == 8'd0) begin\n        if (v[10:3] == 8'd0) begin\n          if (v[2:1] == 2'd0) c = v[0] ? 5'd18 : 5'd19;\n          else                c = v[2] ? 5'd16 : 5'd17;\n        end else begin\n          if (v[10:7] == 4'd0) begin\n            if (v[6:5] == 2'd0) c = v[4] ? 5'd14 : 5'd15;\n            else                c = v[6] ? 5'd12 : 5'd13;\n          end else begin\n            if (v[10:9] == 2'd0) c = v[8]  ? 5'd10 : 5'd11;\n            else                 c = v[10] ? 5'd8  : 5'd9;\n          end\n        end\n      end else begin\n        if (v[18:15] == 4'd0) begin\n          if (v[14:13] == 2'd0) c = v[12] ? 5'd6 : 5'd7;\n          else                  c = v[14] ? 5'd4 : 5'd5;\n        end else begin\n          if (v[18:17] == 2'd0) c = v[16] ? 5'd2 : 5'd3;\n          else                  c = v[18] ? 5'd0 : 5'd1;\n        end\n      end\n      lzc19 = c;\n    end\n  endfunction\n\n  // 8-bit posit(8,2) decoder -> {sign, rg_exp[5:0], mant[3:0]}\n  function automatic [10:0] dec8 (input [7:0] p);\n    logic              sgn;\n    logic [6:0]        m, mv, lzop, op;\n    logic              regs, emp;\n    logic [3:0]        lz;\n    logic [2:0]        rl, rb;\n    logic signed [3:0] rk;\n    logic [5:0]        rge;\n    begin\n      sgn  = p[7];\n      m    = p[6:0];\n      mv   = sgn ? (7'd0 - m) : m;\n      regs = mv[6];\n      lzop = regs ? ~mv : mv;\n      emp  = (lzop == 7'd0);\n      lz   = lzc8({1'b0, lzop}) - 4'd1;\n      rl   = emp ? 3'd7 : lz[2:0];\n      rb   = emp ? 3'd7 : (lz[2:0] + 3'd1);\n      rk   = regs ? {1'b0, (rl - 3'd1)} : {1'b1, (~rl + 3'd1)};\n      op   = mv << rb;\n      rge  = {{2{rk[3]}}, rk} <<< 2;\n      dec8 = {sgn, (rge | {4'b0, op[6:5]}), {|m, op[4:2]}};\n    end\n  endfunction\n\n  // 16-bit posit(16,2) decoder -> {sign, rg_exp[6:0], mant[11:0]}\n  function automatic [19:0] dec16 (input [15:0] p);\n    logic               sgn;\n    logic [14:0]        m, mv, lzop, op;\n    logic               regs, emp;\n    logic [4:0]         lz;\n    logic [3:0]         rl, rb;\n    logic signed [4:0]  rk;\n    logic [6:0]         rge;\n    begin\n      sgn  = p[15];\n      m    = p[14:0];\n      mv   = sgn ? (15'd0 - m) : m;\n      regs = mv[14];\n      lzop = regs ? ~mv : mv;\n      emp  = (lzop == 15'd0);\n      lz   = lzc16({1'b0, lzop}) - 5'd1;\n      rl   = emp ? 4'd15 : lz[3:0];\n      rb   = emp ? 4'd15 : (lz[3:0] + 4'd1);\n      rk   = regs ? {1'b0, (rl - 4'd1)} : {1'b1, (~rl + 4'd1)};\n      op   = mv << rb;\n      rge  = {{2{rk[4]}}, rk} <<< 2;\n      dec16 = {sgn, (rge | {5'b0, op[14:13]}), {|m, op[12:2]}};\n    end\n  endfunction\n\n  function automatic signed [EW-1:0] smax (input signed [EW-1:0] x,\n                                           input signed [EW-1:0] y);\n    smax = (x > y) ? x : y;\n  endfunction\n\n  // =====================================================================\n  // Stage 1 : posit decode\n  // =====================================================================\n  wire [N-1:0]     sgn_ab;\n  wire [N*EW-1:0]  exp_c;\n  wire [N*MI-1:0]  mant_a, mant_b;\n\n  genvar i;\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < N; i++) begin : g_dec\n      wire [10:0] da, db;\n      assign da = dec8(operands_a[i*n_i +: n_i]);\n      assign db = dec8(operands_b[i*n_i +: n_i]);\n      assign sgn_ab[i]          = da[10] ^ db[10];\n      assign exp_c[i*EW +: EW]  = $signed(da[9:4]) + $signed(db[9:4]);\n      assign mant_a[i*MI +: MI] = da[3:0];\n      assign mant_b[i*MI +: MI] = db[3:0];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  wire [19:0] dacc;\n  assign dacc = dec16(acc);\n\n  logic [N:0]      p1_sgn;\n  logic [N*EW-1:0] p1_exp_c;\n  logic [N*MI-1:0] p1_mant_a, p1_mant_b;\n  logic [EW-1:0]   p1_exp_acc;\n  logic [MO-1:0]   p1_mant_acc;\n\n  always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n    p1_sgn      <= {dacc[19], sgn_ab};\n    p1_exp_c    <= exp_c;\n    p1_mant_a   <= mant_a;\n    p1_mant_b   <= mant_b;\n    p1_exp_acc  <= dacc[18:12];\n    p1_mant_acc <= dacc[11:0];\n  end\n\n  // =====================================================================\n  // Stage 2 : lane multiply + exponent max tree\n  // =====================================================================\n  wire [N*MULW-1:0] prod;\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < N; i++) begin : g_mul\n      assign prod[i*MULW +: MULW] = p1_mant_a[i*MI +: MI] * p1_mant_b[i*MI +: MI];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  wire signed [NT*EW-1:0] ei;\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < N; i++) begin : g_ei\n      assign ei[i*EW +: EW] = p1_exp_c[i*EW +: EW];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n  assign ei[N*EW +: EW] = p1_exp_acc;\n\n  wire signed [9*EW-1:0] l1;\n  wire signed [5*EW-1:0] l2;\n  wire signed [3*EW-1:0] l3;\n  wire signed [2*EW-1:0] l4;\n  wire signed [EW-1:0]   emax;\n\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < 8; i++) begin : g_m1\n      assign l1[i*EW +: EW] = smax(ei[(2*i)*EW +: EW], ei[(2*i+1)*EW +: EW]);\n    end\n  endgenerate\n  assign l1[8*EW +: EW] = ei[N*EW +: EW];\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < 4; i++) begin : g_m2\n      assign l2[i*EW +: EW] = smax(l1[(2*i)*EW +: EW], l1[(2*i+1)*EW +: EW]);\n    end\n  endgenerate\n  assign l2[4*EW +: EW] = l1[8*EW +: EW];\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < 2; i++) begin : g_m3\n      assign l3[i*EW +: EW] = smax(l2[(2*i)*EW +: EW], l2[(2*i+1)*EW +: EW]);\n    end\n  endgenerate\n  assign l3[2*EW +: EW] = l2[4*EW +: EW];\n  assign l4[0*EW +: EW] = smax(l3[0*EW +: EW], l3[1*EW +: EW]);\n  assign l4[1*EW +: EW] = l3[2*EW +: EW];\n  assign emax = smax(l4[0*EW +: EW], l4[1*EW +: EW]);\n\n  logic [N:0]              p2_sgn;\n  logic signed [EW-1:0]    p2_emax;\n  logic [N*MULW-1:0]       p2_prod;\n  logic [MO-1:0]           p2_mant_acc;\n  logic signed [NT*EW-1:0] p2_ei;\n\n  always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n    p2_sgn      <= p1_sgn;\n    p2_emax     <= emax;\n    p2_prod     <= prod;\n    p2_mant_acc <= p1_mant_acc;\n    p2_ei       <= ei;\n  end\n\n  // =====================================================================\n  // Stage 3 : exponent difference + alignment shift (magnitude only)\n  // =====================================================================\n  wire [NT*AW-1:0] aval;\n\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < NT; i++) begin : g_align\n      wire signed [7:0] diff;\n      wire [3:0]        sh;\n      wire [AW-1:0]     pfull;\n      assign diff = {p2_emax[EW-1], p2_emax} - {p2_ei[i*EW+EW-1], p2_ei[i*EW +: EW]};\n      assign sh   = (|diff[7:4]) ? 4'd14 : diff[3:0];\n      if (i < N) begin : g_lane\n        assign pfull = {p2_prod[i*MULW +: MULW], 6'b0};\n      end\n      else begin : g_acc\n        assign pfull = {1'b0, p2_mant_acc, 1'b0};\n      end\n      assign aval[i*AW +: AW] = pfull >> sh;\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  logic signed [EW-1:0] p3_emax;\n  logic [NT*AW-1:0]     p3_aval;\n  logic [N:0]           p3_neg;\n\n  always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n    p3_emax <= p2_emax;\n    p3_aval <= aval;\n    p3_neg  <= p2_sgn;\n  end\n\n  // =====================================================================\n  // Stage 4 : two's-complement accumulation\n  //   sum = SUM_i (a_i XOR s_i) + popcount(s)\n  // =====================================================================\n  wire [NT*AW-1:0] mval;\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < 8; i++) begin : g_pp\n      assign pp[i] = {1'b0, p3_neg[2*i]} + {1'b0, p3_neg[2*i+1]};\n    end\n  endgenerate\n  assign pp[8] = {1'b0, p3_neg[16]};\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < 4; i++) begin : g_pq\n      assign pq[i] = {1'b0, pp[2*i]} + {1'b0, pp[2*i+1]};\n    end\n  endgenerate\n  assign pq[4] = {1'b0, pp[8]};\n  assign pr[0] = {1'b0, pq[0]} + {1'b0, pq[1]};\n  assign pr[1] = {1'b0, pq[2]} + {1'b0, pq[3]};\n  assign pr[2] = {1'b0, pq[4]};\n  assign pop = ({1'b0, pr[0]} + {1'b0, pr[1]}) + {1'b0, pr[2]};\n\n  wire [14:0] s1 [0:8];\n  wire [15:0] s2 [0:4];\n  wire [16:0] s3 [0:2];\n  wire [17:0] s4 [0:1];\n  wire [18:0] s5;\n\n  generate\n    for (i = 0;   -> create_file(content='// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Pipelined 16-lane posit(8,2) dot-product-accumulate unit.\n// Bit-exact re-implementation of the reference pdpu_top_pipelined arithmetic\n// with a compact datapath and 5 register stages (pipe1..pipe5).\n//\n// Optimizations vs. the reference\n//  * decoded exponent field kept at 7 bits, mantissas reduced to the bits that\n//    actually reach the output (4-bit input mantissas, 8x8 lane product);\n//  * the accumulator uses the classic "complement with the sign mask" trick,\n//    so no per-lane conditional two\'s-complement negation is needed:\n//    mval = {sign,..,sign, a^sign} == (sign ? 2^20-1-a : a);\n//  * saturating clamp of the alignment shift amount;\n//  * the posit encoder\'s variable right shift is expressed as one 31-bit\n//    variable-position window read instead of a 46-bit barrel shifter.\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n`default_nettype none\n\nmodule pdpu_top_pipelined #(\n    parameter int unsigned N           = 16,\n    parameter int unsigned n_i         = 8,\n    parameter int unsigned es_i        = 2,\n    parameter int unsigned n_o         = 16,\n    parameter int unsigned es_o        = 2,\n    parameter int unsigned ALIGN_WIDTH = 14\n)(\n    input  logic             clk,\n    input  logic [N*n_i-1:0] operands_a,\n    input  logic [N*n_i-1:0] operands_b,\n    input  logic [n_o-1:0]   acc,\n    output logic [n_o-1:0]   result_o\n);\n\n  localparam int unsigned EW   = 7; i < N; i++) begin : g_dec\n      wire [10:0] da, db;\n      assign da = dec8(operands_a[i*n_i +: n_i]);\n      assign db = dec8(operands_b[i*n_i +: n_i]);\n      assign sgn_ab[i]          = da[10] ^ db[10];\n      assign exp_c[i*EW +: EW]  = $signed(da[9:4]) + $signed(db[9:4]);\n      assign mant_a[i*MI +: MI] = da[3:0];\n      assign mant_b[i*MI +: MI] = db[3:0];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  wire [19:0] dacc;\n  assign dacc = dec16(acc);\n\n  logic [N:0]      p1_sgn;\n  logic [N*EW-1:0] p1_exp_c;\n  logic [N*MI-1:0] p1_mant_a, p1_mant_b;\n  logic [EW-1:0]   p1_exp_acc;\n  logic [MO-1:0]   p1_mant_acc;\n\n  always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n    p1_sgn      <= {dacc[19], sgn_ab};\n    p1_exp_c    <= exp_c;\n    p1_mant_a   <= mant_a;\n    p1_mant_b   <= mant_b;\n    p1_exp_acc  <= dacc[18:12];\n    p1_mant_acc <= dacc[11:0];\n  end\n\n  // =====================================================================\n  // Stage 2 : lane multiply + exponent max tree\n  // =====================================================================\n  wire [N*MULW-1:0] prod;\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i++) begin : g_ei\n      assign ei[i*EW +: EW] = p1_exp_c[i*EW +: EW];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n  assign ei[N*EW +: EW] = p1_exp_acc;\n\n  wire signed [9*EW-1:0] l1;\n  wire signed [5*EW-1:0] l2;\n  wire signed [3*EW-1:0] l3;\n  wire signed [2*EW-1:0] l4;\n  wire signed [EW-1:0]   emax;\n\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < 8; i++) begin : g_m1\n      assign l1[i*EW +: EW] = smax(ei[(2*i)*EW +: EW], ei[(2*i+1)*EW +: EW]);\n    end\n  endgenerate\n  assign l1[8*EW +: EW] = ei[N*EW +: EW];\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < 4; i++) begin : g_m2\n      assign l2[i*EW +: EW] = smax(l1[(2*i)*EW +: EW], l1[(2*i+1)*EW +: EW]);\n    end\n  endgenerate\n  assign l2[4*EW +: EW] = l1[8*EW +: EW];\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < 2; i++) begin : g_m3\n      assign l3[i*EW +: EW] = smax(l2[(2*i)*EW +: EW], l2[(2*i+1)*EW +: EW]);\n    end\n  endgenerate\n  assign l3[2*EW +: EW] = l2[4*EW +: EW];\n  assign l4[0*EW +: EW] = smax(l3[0*EW +: EW], l3[1*EW +: EW]);\n  assign l4[1*EW +: EW] = l3[2*EW +: EW];\n  assign emax = smax(l4[0*EW +: EW], l4[1*EW +: EW]);\n\n  logic [N:0]              p2_sgn;\n  logic signed [EW-1:0]    p2_emax;\n  logic [N*MULW-1:0]       p2_prod;\n  logic [MO-1:0]           p2_mant_acc;\n  logic signed [NT*EW-1:0] p2_ei;\n\n  always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n    p2_sgn      <= p1_sgn;\n    p2_emax     <= emax;\n    p2_prod     <= prod;\n    p2_mant_acc <= p1_mant_acc;\n    p2_ei       <= ei;\n  end\n\n  // =====================================================================\n  // Stage 3 : exponent difference + alignment shift (magnitude only)\n  // =====================================================================\n  wire [NT*AW-1:0] aval;\n\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < NT; i++) begin : g_align\n      wire signed [7:0] diff;\n      wire [3:0]        sh;\n      wire [AW-1:0]     pfull;\n      assign diff = {p2_emax[EW-1], p2_emax} - {p2_ei[i*EW+EW-1], p2_ei[i*EW +: EW]};\n      assign sh   = (|diff[7:4]) ? 4\'d14 : diff[3:0];\n      if (i < N) begin : g_lane\n        assign pfull = {p2_prod[i*MULW +: MULW], 6\'b0};\n      end\n      else begin : g_acc\n        assign pfull = {1\'b0, p2_mant_acc, 1\'b0};\n      end\n      assign aval[i*AW +: AW] = pfull >> sh;\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  logic signed [EW-1:0] p3_emax;\n  logic [NT*AW-1:0]     p3_aval;\n  logic [N:0]           p3_neg;\n\n  always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n    p3_emax <= p2_emax;\n    p3_aval <= aval;\n    p3_neg  <= p2_sgn;\n  end\n\n  // =====================================================================\n  // Stage 4 : accumulation\n  //   mval_i = {6{sign_i}, a_i ^ {14{sign_i}}}  ==  sign ? 2^20-1-a_i : a_i\n  //   total  = SUM mval_i (mod 2^20) == two\'s complement signed sum\n  // =====================================================================\n  wire [NT*SUML-1:0] mval;\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < NT; i++) begin : g_mask\n      assign mval[i*SUML +: SUML] = { {6{p3_neg[i]}},\n                                      p3_aval[i*AW +: AW] ^ {AW{p3_neg[i]}} };\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  wire [SUML-1:0] s1 [0:8];\n  wire [SUML-1:0] s2 [0:4];\n  wire [SUML-1:0] s3 [0:2];\n  wire [SUML-1:0] s4 [0:1];\n  wire [SUML-1:0] total;\n\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < 8; i++) begin : g_s1\n      assign s1[i] = mval[(2*i)*SUML +: SUML] + mval[(2*i+1)*SUML +: SUML];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n  assign s1[8] = mval[16*SUML +: SUML];\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < 4; ((~regime_k) + 5\'d2) : (regime_k + 5\'d2);\n  wire [4:0]        sa          = (regime_bits >= 5\'d16) ? 5\'d16 : regime_bits;\n  wire [4:0]        sk          = sa - 5\'d1;               // 1..15\n\n  // W[j] == rg_exp_mant[j] of the reference encoder; W[30] = 0\n  wire [30:0] W  = {1\'b0, R, {14{~R}}, fexp[1:0], fmant[12:0]};\n  wire [30:0] Y  = W >> sk;                 // shift by sa-1\n  wire [14:0] vbr   = Y[15:1];\n  wire        rbit  = Y[0];\n  wire [15:0] emask = (16\'d1 << sk) - 16\'d1;\n  wire        sticky = |(W[14:0] & emask[14:0]);\n  wire        rval  = rbit & (sticky | vbr[0]);\n  wire [14:0] vaf   = vbr + {14\'d0, rval};\n\n  logic        p5_sgn, p5_nz;\n  logic [14:0] p5_vaf;\n\n  always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n    p5_sgn <= p4_sgn;\n    p5_nz  <= fmant[13];\n    p5_vaf <= vaf;\n  end\n\n  wire [14:0] neg_val = (~p5_vaf) + 15\'d1;\n  assign result_o = p5_nz ? (p5_sgn ? {1\'b1, neg_val} : {1\'b0, p5_vaf})\n                          : {n_o{1\'b0}};\n\nendmodule\n\n`default_nettype wire\n', filename='design5.sv')

  -> create_file(content='// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Pipelined 16-lane posit(8,2) dot-product-accumulate unit.\n// Bit-exact re-implementation of the reference pdpu_top_pipelined arithmetic\n// with a compact datapath and 5 register stages (pipe1..pipe5).\n//\n// Optimizations vs. the reference\n//  * decoded exponent field kept at 7 bits, mantissas reduced to the bits that\n//    actually reach the output (4-bit input mantissas, 8x8 lane product);\n//  * the accumulator uses the "complement with the sign mask" identity, so no\n//    per-lane conditional two\'s-complement negation is needed:\n//       neg ? 2^20-1-a : a   ==   {6{neg}, a ^ {14{neg}}}\n//    with the accumulated +1 per negative folded in as a sign-bit popcount;\n//  * saturating clamp of the alignment shift amount;\n//  * the posit encoder\'s variable right shift is expressed as one 31-bit\n//    variable-position window read instead of a 46-bit barrel shifter.\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n`default_nettype none\n\nmodule pdpu_top_pipelined #(\n    parameter int unsigned N           = 16,\n    parameter int unsigned n_i         = 8,\n    parameter int unsigned es_i        = 2,\n    parameter int unsigned n_o         = 16,\n    parameter int unsigned es_o        = 2,\n    parameter int unsigned ALIGN_WIDTH = 14\n)(\n    input  logic             clk,\n    input  logic [N*n_i-1:0] operands_a,\n    input  logic [N*n_i-1:0] operands_b,\n    input  logic [n_o-1:0]   acc,\n    output logic [n_o-1:0]   result_o\n);\n\n  localparam int unsigned EW   = 7;             // signed exponent field\n  localparam int unsigned MI   = 4;             // input mantissa incl. hidden bit\n  localparam int unsigned MULW = 8;             // 4x4 lane product\n  localparam int unsigned MO   = 12;            // acc mantissa incl. hidden bit\n  localparam int unsigned AW   = ALIGN_WIDTH;   // 14\n  localparam int unsigned SUML = 20;            // accumulator width\n  localparam int unsigned ML   = 19;            // result magnitude width\n  localparam int unsigned NT   = N + 1;         // 17 summands\n\n  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n  // helper functions\n  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n  function automatic [3:0] lzc8 (input [7:0] v);\n    logic [3:0] c;\n    logic [7:0] t;\n    begin\n      c = 4\'d0; t = v;\n      if (t[7:4] == 4\'d0) begin c = c + 4\'d4; t = t << 4; end\n      if (t[7:6] == 2\'d0) begin c = c + 4\'d2; t = t << 2; end\n      if (t[7]   == 1\'b0) begin c = c + 4\'d1; t = t << 1; end\n      if (t[7]   == 1\'b0) c = c + 4\'d1;\n      lzc8 = c;\n    end\n  endfunction\n\n  function automatic [4:0] lzc16 (input [15:0] v);\n    logic [4:0]  c;\n    logic [15:0] t;\n    begin\n      c = 5\'d0; t = v;\n      if (t[15:8]  == 8\'d0) begin c = c + 5\'d8; t = t << 8; end\n      if (t[15:12] == 4\'d0) begin c = c + 5\'d4; t = t << 4; end\n      if (t[15:14] == 2\'d0) begin c = c + 5\'d2; t = t << 2; end\n      if (t[15]    == 1\'b0) begin c = c + 5\'d1; t = t << 1; end\n      if (t[15]    == 1\'b0) c = c + 5\'d1;\n      lzc16 = c;\n    end\n  endfunction\n\n  // leading-zero count of a 19-bit vector (0..19)\n  function automatic [4:0] lzc19 (input [18:0] v);\n    logic [4:0] c;\n    begin\n      if (v[18:11] == 8\'d0) begin\n        if (v[10:3] == 8\'d0) begin\n          if (v[2:1] == 2\'d0) c = v[0] ? 5\'d18 : 5\'d19;\n          else                c = v[2] ? 5\'d16 : 5\'d17;\n        end else begin\n          if (v[10:7] == 4\'d0) begin\n            if (v[6:5] == 2\'d0) c = v[4] ? 5\'d14 : 5\'d15;\n            else                c = v[6] ? 5\'d12 : 5\'d13;\n          end else begin\n            if (v[10:9] == 2\'d0) c = v[8]  ? 5\'d10 : 5\'d11;\n            else                 c = v[10] ? 5\'d8  : 5\'d9;\n          end\n        end\n      end else begin\n        if (v[18:15] == 4\'d0) begin\n          if (v[14:13] == 2\'d0) c = v[12] ? 5\'d6 : 5\'d7;\n          else                  c = v[14] ? 5\'d4 : 5\'d5;\n        end else begin\n          if (v[18:17] == 2\'d0) c = v[16] ? 5\'d2 : 5\'d3;\n          else                  c = v[18] ? 5\'d0 : 5\'d1;\n        end\n      end\n      lzc19 = c;\n    end\n  endfunction\n\n  // 8-bit posit(8,2) decoder -> {sign, rg_exp[5:0], mant[3:0]}\n  function automatic [10:0] dec8 (input [7:0] p);\n    logic              sgn;\n    logic [6:0]        m, mv, lzop, op;\n    logic              regs, emp;\n    logic [3:0]        lz;\n    logic [2:0]        rl, rb;\n    logic signed [3:0] rk;\n    logic [5:0]        rge;\n    begin\n      sgn  = p[7];\n      m    = p[6:0];\n      mv   = sgn ? (7\'d0 - m) : m;\n      regs = mv[6];\n      lzop = regs ? ~mv : mv;\n      emp  = (lzop == 7\'d0);\n      lz   = lzc8({1\'b0, lzop}) - 4\'d1;\n      rl   = emp ? 3\'d7 : lz[2:0];\n      rb   = emp ? 3\'d7 : (lz[2:0] + 3\'d1);\n      rk   = regs ? {1\'b0, (rl - 3\'d1)} : {1\'b1, (~rl + 3\'d1)};\n      op   = mv << rb;\n      rge  = {{2{rk[3]}}, rk} <<< 2;\n      dec8 = {sgn, (rge | {4\'b0, op[6:5]}), {|m, op[4:2]}};\n    end\n  endfunction\n\n  // 16-bit posit(16,2) decoder -> {sign, rg_exp[6:0], mant[11:0]}\n  function automatic [19:0] dec16 (input [15:0] p);\n    logic               sgn;\n    logic [14:0]        m, mv, lzop, op;\n    logic               regs, emp;\n    logic [4:0]         lz;\n    logic [3:0]         rl, rb;\n    logic signed [4:0]  rk;\n    logic [6:0]         rge;\n    begin\n      sgn  = p[15];\n      m    = p[14:0];\n      mv   = sgn ? (15\'d0 - m) : m;\n      regs = mv[14];\n      lzop = regs ? ~mv : mv;\n      emp  = (lzop == 15\'d0);\n      lz   = lzc16({1\'b0, lzop}) - 5\'d1;\n      rl   = emp ? 4\'d15 : lz[3:0];\n      rb   = emp ? 4\'d15 : (lz[3:0] + 4\'d1);\n      rk   = regs ? {1\'b0, (rl - 4\'d1)} : {1\'b1, (~rl + 4\'d1)};\n      op   = mv << rb;\n      rge  = {{2{rk[4]}}, rk} <<< 2;\n      dec16 = {sgn, (rge | {5\'b0, op[14:13]}), {|m, op[12:2]}};\n    end\n  endfunction\n\n  function automatic signed [EW-1:0] smax (input signed [EW-1:0] x,\n                                           input signed [EW-1:0] y);\n    smax = (x > y) ? x : y;\n  endfunction\n\n  // =====================================================================\n  // Stage 1 : posit decode\n  // =====================================================================\n  wire [N-1:0]     sgn_ab;\n  wire [N*EW-1:0]  exp_c;\n  wire [N*MI-1:0]  mant_a, mant_b;\n\n  genvar i;\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < N; i++) begin : g_mul\n      assign prod[i*MULW +: MULW] = p1_mant_a[i*MI +: MI] * p1_mant_b[i*MI +: MI];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  wire signed [NT*EW-1:0] ei;\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < N; i++) begin : g_ei\n      assign ei[i*EW +: EW] = p1_exp_c[i*EW +: EW];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n  assign ei[N*EW +: EW] = p1_exp_acc;\n\n  wire signed [9*EW-1:0] l1;\n  wire signed [5*EW-1:0] l2;\n  wire signed [3*EW-1:0] l3;\n  wire signed [2*EW-1:0] l4;\n  wire signed [EW-1:0]   emax;\n\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < 8; i++) begin : g_m1\n      assign l1[i*EW +: EW] = smax(ei[(2*i)*EW +: EW], ei[(2*i+1)*EW +: EW]);\n    end\n  endgenerate\n  assign l1[8*EW +: EW] = ei[N*EW +: EW];\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < 4; i++) begin : g_m2\n      assign l2[i*EW +: EW] = smax(l1[(2*i)*EW +: EW], l1[(2*i+1)*EW +: EW]);\n    end\n  endgenerate\n  assign l2[4*EW +: EW] = l1[8*EW +: EW];\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < NT; i++) begin : g_align\n      wire signed [7:0] diff;\n      wire [3:0]        sh;\n      wire [AW-1:0]     pfull;\n      assign diff = {p2_emax[EW-1], p2_emax} - {p2_ei[i*EW+EW-1], p2_ei[i*EW +: EW]};\n      assign sh   = (|diff[7:4]) ? 4\'d14 : diff[3:0];\n      if (i < N) begin : g_lane\n        assign pfull = {p2_prod[i*MULW +: MULW], 6\'b0};\n      end\n      else begin : g_acc\n        assign pfull = {1\'b0, p2_mant_acc, 1\'b0};\n      end\n      assign aval[i*AW +: AW] = pfull >> sh;\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  logic signed [EW-1:0] p3_emax;\n  logic [NT*AW-1:0]     p3_aval;\n  logic [N:0]           p3_neg;\n\n  always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n    p3_emax <= p2_emax;\n    p3_aval <= aval;\n    p3_neg  <= p2_sgn;\n  end\n\n  // =====================================================================\n  // Stage 4 : accumulation\n  //   mval_i = {6{neg_i}, a_i ^ {14{neg_i}}}  ==  neg ? 2^20-1-a : a\n  //   total  = SUM_i mval_i + popcount(neg)   ==  signed two\'s complement sum\n  // =====================================================================\n  wire [NT*SUML-1:0] mval;\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < N; i++) begin : g_mul\n      assign prod[i*MULW +: MULW] = p1_mant_a[i*MI +: MI] * p1_mant_b[i*MI +: MI];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  wire signed [NT*EW-1:0] ei;\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < N; i++) begin : g_ei\n      assign ei[i*EW +: EW] = p1_exp_c[i*EW +: EW];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n  assign ei[N*EW +: EW] = p1_exp_acc;\n\n  wire signed [9*EW-1:0] l1;\n  wire signed [5*EW-1:0] l2;\n  wire signed [3*EW-1:0] l3;\n  wire signed [2*EW-1:0] l4;\n  wire signed [EW-1:0]   emax;\n\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < 8; i++) begin : g_m1\n      assign l1[i*EW +: EW] = smax(ei[(2*i)*EW +: EW], ei[(2*i+1)*EW +: EW]);\n    end\n  endgenerate\n  assign l1[8*EW +: EW] = ei[N*EW +: EW];\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < 4; i++) begin : g_m2\n      assign l2[i*EW +: EW] = smax(l1[(2*i)*EW +: EW], l1[(2*i+1)*EW +: EW]);\n    end\n  endgenerate\n  assign l2[4*EW +: EW] = l1[8*EW +: EW];\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < 2; i++) begin : g_m3\n      assign l3[i*EW +: EW] = smax(l2[(2*i)*EW +: EW], l2[(2*i+1)*EW +: EW]);\n    end\n  endgenerate\n  assign l3[2*EW +: EW] = l2[4*EW +: EW];\n  assign l4[0*EW +: EW] = smax(l3[0*EW +: EW], l3[1*EW +: EW]);\n  assign l4[1*EW +: EW] = l3[2*EW +: EW];\n  assign emax = smax(l4[0*EW +: EW], l4[1*EW +: EW]);\n\n  logic [N:0]              p2_sgn;\n  logic signed [EW-1:0]    p2_emax;\n  logic [N*MULW-1:0]       p2_prod;\n  logic [MO-1:0]           p2_mant_acc;\n  logic signed [NT*EW-1:0] p2_ei;\n\n  always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n    p2_sgn      <= p1_sgn;\n    p2_emax     <= emax;\n    p2_prod     <= prod;\n    p2_mant_acc <= p1_mant_acc;\n    p2_ei       <= ei;\n  end\n\n  // =====================================================================\n  // Stage 3 : exponent difference + alignment shift (magnitude only)\n  // =====================================================================\n  wire [NT*AW-1:0] aval;\n\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < NT; i++) begin : g_align\n      wire signed [7:0] diff;\n      wire [3:0]        sh;\n      wire [AW-1:0]     pfull;\n      assign diff = {p2_emax[EW-1], p2_emax} - {p2_ei[i*EW+EW-1], p2_ei[i*EW +: EW]};\n      assign sh   = (|diff[7:4]) ? 4'd14 : diff[3:0];\n      if (i < N) begin : g_lane\n        assign pfull = {p2_prod[i*MULW +: MULW], 6'b0};\n      end\n      else begin : g_acc\n        assign pfull = {1'b0, p2_mant_acc, 1'b0};\n      end\n      assign aval[i*AW +: AW] = pfull >> sh;\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  logic signed [EW-1:0] p3_emax;\n  logic [NT*AW-1:0]     p3_aval;\n  logic [N:0]           p3_neg;\n\n  always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n    p3_emax <= p2_emax;\n    p3_aval <= aval;\n    p3_neg  <= p2_sgn;\n  end\n\n  // =====================================================================\n  // Stage 4 : accumulation\n  //   mval_i = {6{neg_i}, a_i ^ {14{neg_i}}}  ==  neg ? 2^20-1-a : a\n  //   total  = SUM_i mval_i + popcount(neg)   ==  signed two's complement sum\n  // =====================================================================\n  wire [NT*SUML-1:0] mval;\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < NT; i++) begin : g_mask\n      assign mval[i*SUML +: SUML] = { {6{p3_neg[i]}},\n                                      p3_aval[i*AW +: AW] ^ {AW{p3_neg[i]}} };\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  wire [1:0] pp [0:8];\n  wire [2:0] pq [0:4];\n  wire [3:0] pr [0:2];\n  wire [4:0] pop;\n\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < 8; i++) begin : g_pp\n      assign pp[i] = {1'b0, p3_neg[2*i]} + {1'b0, p3_neg[2*i+1]};\n    end\n  endgenerate\n  assign pp[8] = {1'b0, p3_neg[16]};\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < 4; i++) begin : g_pq\n      assign pq[i] = {1'b0, pp[2*i]} + {1'b0, pp[2*i+1]};\n    end\n  endgenerate\n  assign pq[4] = {1'b0, pp[8]};\n  assign pr[0] = {1'b0, pq[0]} + {1'b0, pq[1]};\n  assign pr[1] = {1'b0, pq[2]} + {1'b0, pq[3]};\n  assign pr[2] = {1'b0, pq[4]};\n  assign pop = ({1'b0, pr[0]} + {1'b0, pr[1]}) + {1'b0, pr[2]};\n\n  wire [SUML-1:0] s1 [0:8];\n  wire [SUML-1:0] s2 [0:4];\n  wire [SUML-1:0] s3 [0:2];\n  wire [SUML-1:0] s4 [0:1];\n  wire [SUML-1:0] total;\n\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < 8; i++) begin : g_s1\n      assign s1[i] = mval[(2*i)*SUML +: SUML] + mval[(2*i+1)*SUML +: SUML];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n  assign s1[8] = mval[16*SUML +: SUML];\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < 4; i++) begin : g_s2\n      assign s2[i] = s1[2*i] + s1[2*i+1];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n  assign s2[4] = s1[8];\n  assign s3[0] = s2[0] + s2[1];\n  assign s3[1] = s2[2] + s2[3];\n  assign s3[2] = s2[4];\n  assign s4[0] = s3[0] + s3[1];\n  assign s4[1] = s3[2];\n  assign total = (s4[0] + s4[1]) + {{(SUML-5){1'b0}}, pop};\n\n  wire          sum_sgn = total[SUML-1];\n  wire [ML-1:0] mag     = sum_sgn ? (~total[ML-1:0] + 1'b1) : total[ML-1:0];\n\n  logic signed [EW-1:0] p4_emax;\n  logic                 p4_sgn;\n  logic [ML-1:0]        p4_mag;\n\n  always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n    p4_emax <= p3_emax;\n    p4_sgn  <= sum_sgn;\n    p4_mag  <= mag;\n  end\n\n  // =====================================================================\n  // Stage 5 : normalization + posit encoder\n  // =====================================================================\n  wire [4:0]        lz     = lzc19(p4_mag);\n  wire [ML-1:0]     norm   = p4_mag << lz;\n  wire [13:0]       fmant  = {norm[ML-1:6], |norm[5:0]};   // 14 bits\n  wire signed [7:0] fexp8  = $signed({{1{p4_emax[EW-1]}}, p4_emax})\n                           + 8'sd6 - $signed({3'b0, lz});\n  wire [6:0]        fexp   = fexp8[6:0];\n\n  wire              R           = fexp[6];\n  wire signed [4:0] regime_k    = fexp[6:2];\n  wire [4:0]        regime_bits = R ? ((~regime_k) + 5'd2) : (regime_k + 5'd2);\n  wire [4:0]        sa          = (regime_bits >= 5'd16) ? 5'd16 : regime_bits;\n\n  // rg_exp_mant of the reference encoder, zero-extended to 31 bits\n  wire [14:0]       regime = R ? 15'd1 : ~(15'd1);\n  wire [30:0]       W      = {1'b0, fmant[12:0], fexp[1:0], regime};\n  wire [30:0]       Y      = W >> sa;\n  wire [14:0]       vbr    = Y[14:0];\n  wire              rbit   = W[sa - 5'd1];\n  wire [30:0]       lomask = (31'd1 << (sa - 5'd1)) - 31'd1;\n  wire              sticky = |(W & lomask);\n  wire              rval   = rbit & (sticky | vbr[0]);\n  wire [14:0]       vaf    = vbr + {14'd0, rval};\n\n  logic        p5_sgn, p5_nz;\n  logic [14:0] p5_vaf;\n\n  always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n    p5_sgn <= p4_sgn;\n    p5_nz  <= fmant[13];\n    p5_vaf <= vaf;\n  end\n\n  wire [14:0] neg_val = (~p5_vaf) + 15'd1;\n  assign result_o = p5_nz ? (p5_sgn ? {1'b1, neg_val} : {1'b0, p5_vaf})\n                          : {n_o{1'b0}};\n\nendmodule\n\n`default_nettype wire\n", filename='design6.sv')
