[ASSISTANT]
Correct but slower. The stage-4 adder tree is now the bottleneck. Let me replace it with a carry-save tree and prune the multiplier width.




  -> create_file(content='// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Pipelined 16-lane posit(8,2) dot-product-accumulate unit.\n// Bit-exact re-implementation of the reference pdpu_top_pipelined arithmetic\n// with a compact datapath and 5 register stages (pipe1..pipe5).\n//\n// Optimizations vs. the reference\n//  * decoded exponent field kept at 7 bits, mantissas reduced to the bits that\n//    actually reach the output (4-bit input mantissas, 6-bit lane product);\n//  * the accumulator uses the "complement with the sign mask" identity, so no\n//    per-lane conditional two\'s-complement negation is needed:\n//       neg ? 2^20-1-a : a   ==   {6{neg}, a ^ {14{neg}}}\n//    with the accumulated +1 per negative folded in as a sign-bit popcount;\n//  * the 17-operand sum is reduced with a carry-save (3:2) tree, so the depth\n//    is 6 full-adders plus one final carry-propagate add;\n//  * the posit encoder\'s variable right shift is expressed as one 31-bit\n//    variable-position window read instead of a 46-bit barrel shifter.\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n`default_nettype none\n\nmodule pdpu_top_pipelined #(\n    parameter int unsigned N           = 16,\n    parameter int unsigned n_i         = 8,\n    parameter int unsigned es_i        = 2,\n    parameter int unsigned n_o         = 16,\n    parameter int unsigned es_o        = 2,\n    parameter int unsigned ALIGN_WIDTH = 14\n)(\n    input  logic             clk,\n    input  logic [N*n_i-1:0] operands_a,\n    input  logic [N*n_i-1:0] operands_b,\n    input  logic [n_o-1:0]   acc,\n    output logic [n_o-1:0]   result_o\n);\n\n  localparam int unsigned EW   = 7;             // signed exponent field\n  localparam int unsigned MI   = 4;             // input mantissa incl. hidden bit\n  localparam int unsigned PW   = 6;             // lane product bits that matter\n  localparam int unsigned MO   = 12;            // acc mantissa incl. hidden bit\n  localparam int unsigned AW   = ALIGN_WIDTH;   // 14\n  localparam int unsigned SUML = 20;            // accumulator width\n  localparam int unsigned ML   = 19;            // result magnitude width\n  localparam int unsigned NT   = N + 1;         // 17 summands\n\n  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n  // helper functions\n  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n  function automatic [3:0] lzc8 (input [7:0] v);\n    logic [3:0] c;\n    logic [7:0] t;\n    begin\n      c = 4\'d0; t = v;\n      if (t[7:4] == 4\'d0) begin c = c + 4\'d4; t = t << 4; end\n      if (t[7:6] == 2\'d0) begin c = c + 4\'d2; t = t << 2; end\n      if (t[7]   == 1\'b0) begin c = c + 4\'d1; t = t << 1; end\n      if (t[7]   == 1\'b0) c = c + 4\'d1;\n      lzc8 = c;\n    end\n  endfunction\n\n  function automatic [4:0] lzc16 (input [15:0] v);\n    logic [4:0]  c;\n    logic [15:0] t;\n    begin\n      c = 5\'d0; t = v;\n      if (t[15:8]  == 8\'d0) begin c = c + 5\'d8; t = t << 8; end\n      if (t[15:12] == 4\'d0) begin c = c + 5\'d4; t = t << 4; end\n      if (t[15:14] == 2\'d0) begin c = c + 5\'d2; t = t << 2; end\n      if (t[15]    == 1\'b0) begin c = c + 5\'d1; t = t << 1; end\n      if (t[15]    == 1\'b0) c = c + 5\'d1;\n      lzc16 = c;\n    end\n  endfunction\n\n  // leading-zero count of a 19-bit vector (0..19)\n  function automatic [4:0] lzc19 (input [18:0] v);\n    logic [4:0] c;\n    begin\n      if (v[18:11] == 8\'d0) begin\n        if (v[10:3] == 8\'d0) begin\n          if (v[2:1] == 2\'d0) c = v[0] ? 5\'d18 : 5\'d19;\n          else                c = v[2] ? 5\'d16 : 5\'d17;\n        end else begin\n          if (v[10:7] == 4\'d0) begin\n            if (v[6:5] == 2\'d0) c = v[4] ? 5\'d14 : 5\'d15;\n            else                c = v[6] ? 5\'d12 : 5\'d13;\n          end else begin\n            if (v[10:9] == 2\'d0) c = v[8]  ? 5\'d10 : 5\'d11;\n            else                 c = v[10] ? 5\'d8  : 5\'d9;\n          end\n        end\n      end else begin\n        if (v[18:15] == 4\'d0) begin\n          if (v[14:13] == 2\'d0) c = v[12] ? 5\'d6 : 5\'d7;\n          else                  c = v[14] ? 5\'d4 : 5\'d5;\n        end else begin\n          if (v[18:17] == 2\'d0) c = v[16] ? 5\'d2 : 5\'d3;\n          else                  c = v[18] ? 5\'d0 : 5\'d1;\n        end\n      end\n      lzc19 = c;\n    end\n  endfunction\n\n  // 8-bit posit(8,2) decoder -> {sign, rg_exp[5:0], mant[3:0]}\n  function automatic [10:0] dec8 (input [7:0] p);\n    logic              sgn;\n    logic [6:0]        m, mv, lzop, op;\n    logic              regs, emp;\n    logic [3:0]        lz;\n    logic [2:0]        rl, rb;\n    logic signed [3:0] rk;\n    logic [5:0]        rge;\n    begin\n      sgn  = p[7];\n      m    = p[6:0];\n      mv   = sgn ? (7\'d0 - m) : m;\n      regs = mv[6];\n      lzop = regs ? ~mv : mv;\n      emp  = (lzop == 7\'d0);\n      lz   = lzc8({1\'b0, lzop}) - 4\'d1;\n      rl   = emp ? 3\'d7 : lz[2:0];\n      rb   = emp ? 3\'d7 : (lz[2:0] + 3\'d1);\n      rk   = regs ? {1\'b0, (rl - 3\'d1)} : {1\'b1, (~rl + 3\'d1)};\n      op   = mv << rb;\n      rge  = {{2{rk[3]}}, rk} <<< 2;\n      dec8 = {sgn, (rge | {4\'b0, op[6:5]}), {|m, op[4:2]}};\n    end\n  endfunction\n\n  // 16-bit posit(16,2) decoder -> {sign, rg_exp[6:0], mant[11:0]}\n  function automatic [19:0] dec16 (input [15:0] p);\n    logic               sgn;\n    logic [14:0]        m, mv, lzop, op;\n    logic               regs, emp;\n    logic [4:0]         lz;\n    logic [3:0]         rl, rb;\n    logic signed [4:0]  rk;\n    logic [6:0]         rge;\n    begin\n      sgn  = p[15];\n      m    = p[14:0];\n      mv   = sgn ? (15\'d0 - m) : m;\n      regs = mv[14];\n      lzop = regs ? ~mv : mv;\n      emp  = (lzop == 15\'d0);\n      lz   = lzc16({1\'b0, lzop}) - 5\'d1;\n      rl   = emp ? 4\'d15 : lz[3:0];\n      rb   = emp ? 4\'d15 : (lz[3:0] + 4\'d1);\n      rk   = regs ? {1\'b0, (rl - 4\'d1)} : {1\'b1, (~rl + 4\'d1)};\n      op   = mv << rb;\n      rge  = {{2{rk[4]}}, rk} <<< 2;\n      dec16 = {sgn, (rge | {5\'b0, op[14:13]}), {|m, op[12:2]}};\n    end\n  endfunction\n\n  function automatic signed [EW-1:0] smax (input signed [EW-1:0] x,\n                                           input signed [EW-1:0] y);\n    smax = (x > y) ? x : y;\n  endfunction\n\n  // 3:2 compressor returning {carry<<1, sum}\n  function automatic [2*SUML-1:0] csa3 (input [SUML-1:0] x,\n                                        input [SUML-1:0] y,\n                                        input [SUML-1:0] z);\n    logic [SUML-1:0] t, s, c;\n    begin\n      t   = x ^ y;\n      s   = t ^ z;\n      c   = ((x & y) | (z & t)) << 1;\n      csa3 = {c, s};\n    end\n  endfunction\n\n  // =====================================================================\n  // Stage 1 : posit decode\n  // =====================================================================\n  wire [N-1:0]     sgn_ab;\n  wire [N*EW-1:0]  exp_c;\n  wire [N*MI-1:0]  mant_a, mant_b;\n\n  genvar i;\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < N; i++) begin : g_dec\n      wire [10:0] da, db;\n      assign da = dec8(operands_a[i*n_i +: n_i]);\n      assign db = dec8(operands_b[i*n_i +: n_i]);\n      assign sgn_ab[i]          = da[10] ^ db[10];\n      assign exp_c[i*EW +: EW]  = $signed(da[9:4]) + $signed(db[9:4]);\n      assign mant_a[i*MI +: MI] = da[3:0];\n      assign mant_b[i*MI +: MI] = db[3:0];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  wire [19:0] dacc;\n  assign dacc = dec16(acc);\n\n  logic [N:0]      p1_sgn;\n  logic [N*EW-1:0] p1_exp_c;\n  logic [N*MI-1:0] p1_mant_a, p1_mant_b;\n  logic [EW-1:0]   p1_exp_acc;\n  logic [MO-1:0]   p1_mant_acc;\n\n  always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n    p1_sgn      <= {dacc[19], sgn_ab};\n    p1_exp_c    <= exp_c;\n    p1_mant_a   <= mant_a;\n    p1_mant_b   <= mant_b;\n    p1_exp_acc  <= dacc[18:12];\n    p1_mant_acc <= dacc[11:0];\n  end\n\n  // =====================================================================\n  // Stage 2 : lane multiply + exponent max tree\n  // =====================================================================\n  wire [N*PW-1:0] prod;   // top PW bits of the 4x4 product (bits 7:2)\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < N; i++) begin : g_mul\n      assign prod[i*PW +: PW] = (p1_mant_a[i*MI +: MI] * p1_mant_b[i*MI +: MI]) >> 2;\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  wire signed [NT*EW-1:0] ei;\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < N; i++) begin : g_ei\n      assign ei[i*EW +: EW] = p1_exp_c[i*EW +: EW];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n  assign ei[N*EW +: EW] = p1_exp_acc;\n\n  wire signed [9*EW-1:0] l1;\n  wire signed [5*EW-1:0] l2;\n  wire signed [3*EW-1:0] l3;\n  wire signed [2*EW-1:0] l4;\n  wire signed [EW-1:0]   emax;\n\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < 8; i++) begin : g_m1\n      assign l1[i*EW +: EW] = smax(ei[(2*i)*EW +: EW], ei[(2*i+1)*EW +: EW]);\n    end\n  endgenerate\n  assign l1[8*EW +: EW] = ei[N*EW +: EW];\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < 4; i++) begin : g_m2\n      assign l2[i*EW +: EW] = smax(l1[(2*i)*EW +: EW], l1[(2*i+1)*EW +: EW]);\n    end\n  endgenerate\n  assign l2[4*EW +: EW] = l1[8*EW +: EW];\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < 2; i++) begin : g_m3\n      assign l3[i*EW +: EW] = smax(l2[(2*i)*EW +: EW], l2[(2*i+1)*EW +: EW]);\n    end\n  endgenerate\n  assign l3[2*EW +: EW] = l2[4*EW +: EW];\n  assign l4[0*EW +: EW] = smax(l3[0*EW +: EW], l3[1*EW +: EW]);\n  assign l4[1*EW +: EW] = l3[2*EW +: EW];\n  assign emax = smax(l4[0*EW +: EW], l4[1*EW +: EW]);\n\n  logic [N:0]              p2_sgn;\n  logic signed [EW-1:0]    p2_emax;\n  logic [N*PW-1:0]         p2_prod;\n  logic [MO-1:0]           p2_mant_acc;\n  logic signed [NT*EW-1:0] p2_ei;\n\n  always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n    p2_sgn      <= p1_sgn;\n    p2_emax     <= emax;\n    p2_prod     <= prod;\n    p2_mant_acc <= p1_mant_acc;\n    p2_ei       <= ei;\n  end\n\n  // =====================================================================\n  // Stage 3 : exponent difference + alignment shift (magnitude only)\n  // =====================================================================\n  wire [NT*AW-1:0] aval;\n\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < NT; i++) begin : g_align\n      wire signed [7:0] diff;\n      wire [3:0]        sh;\n      wire [AW-1:0]     pfull;\n      assign diff = {p2_emax[EW-1], p2_emax} - {p2_ei[i*EW+EW-1], p2_ei[i*EW +: EW]};\n      assign sh   = (|diff[7:4]) ? 4\'d14 : diff[3:0];\n      if (i < N) begin : g_lane\n        assign pfull = {p2_prod[i*PW +: PW], 8\'b0};\n      end\n      else begin : g_acc\n        assign pfull = {1\'b0, p2_mant_acc, 1\'b0};\n      end\n      assign aval[i*AW +: AW] = pfull >> sh;\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  logic signed [EW-1:0] p3_emax;\n  logic [NT*AW-1:0]     p3_aval;\n  logic [N:0]           p3_neg;\n\n  always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n    p3_emax <= p2_emax;\n    p3_aval <= aval;\n    p3_neg  <= p2_sgn;\n  end\n\n  // =====================================================================\n  // Stage 4 : accumulation\n  //   mval_i = {6{neg_i}, a_i ^ {14{neg_i}}}  ==  neg ? 2^20-1-a : a\n  //   total  = SUM_i mval_i + popcount(neg)   ==  signed two\'s complement sum\n  // =====================================================================\n  wire [SUML-1:0] mv [0:16];\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < 8; i++) begin : g_pp\n      assign pp[i] = {1\'b0, p3_neg[2*i]} + {1\'b0, p3_neg[2*i+1]};\n    end\n  endgenerate\n  assign pp[8] = {1\'b0, p3_neg[16]};\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < 4; i++) begin : g_pq\n      assign pq[i] = {1\'b0, pp[2*i]} + {1\'b0, pp[2*i+1]};\n    end\n  endgenerate\n  assign pq[4] = {1\'b0, pp[8]};\n  assign pr[0] = {1\'b0, pq[0]} + {1\'b0, pq[1]};\n  assign pr[1] = {1\'b0, pq[2]} + {1\'b0, pq[3]};\n  assign pr[2] = {1\'b0, pq[4]};\n  assign pop = ({1\'b0, pr[0]} + {1\'b0, pr[1]}) + {1\'b0, pr[2]};\n\n  // carry-save reduction 17 -> 12 -> 8 -> 6 -> 4 -> 3 -> 2\n  wire [SUML-1:0] a1 [0:11];\n  wire [SUML-1:0] a2 [0:7];\n  wire [SUML-1:0] a3 [0:5];\n  wire [SUML-1:0] a4 [0:3];\n  wire [SUML-1:0] a5 [0:2];\n  wire [SUML-1:0] a6 [0:1];\n\n  wire [2*SUML-1:0] c1 [0:4];\n  wire [2*SUML-1:0] c2 [0:3];\n  wire [2*SUML-1:0] c3 [0:1];\n  wire [2*SUML-1:0] c4 [0:1];\n  wire [2*SUML-1:0] c5;\n  wire [2*SUML-1:0] c6;\n\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < 5; i++) begin : g_c1\n      assign c1[i] = csa3(mv[3*i], mv[3*i+1], mv[3*i+2]);\n      assign a1[2*i]   = c1[i][SUML-1:0];\n      assign a1[2*i+1] = c1[i][2*SUML-1:SUML];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n  assign a1[10] = mv[15];\n  assign a1[11] = mv[16];\n\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < 4; i++) begin : g_c2\n      assign c2[i] = csa3(a1[3*i], a1[3*i+1], a1[3*i+2]);\n      assign a2[2*i]   = c2[i][SUML-1:0];\n      assign a2[2*i+1] = c2[i][2*SUML-1:SUML];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  generate\n    for (i = 0;   -> create_file(content="// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Pipelined 16-lane posit(8,2) dot-product-accumulate unit.\n// Bit-exact re-implementation of the reference pdpu_top_pipelined arithmetic.\n//\n// Stage budget (5 register stages => 6 combinational slots, S0..S5):\n//   S0 -> p1 : posit decode (abs, leading-zero count, regime, mantissa)\n//   S1 -> p2 : exponent add, 17-way max tree, exponent difference, lane mul\n//   S2 -> p3 : alignment shift, sign masking, carry-save reduction (5 levels)\n//   S3 -> p4 : final carry-propagate add, magnitude, leading-zero count\n//   S4 -> p5 : mantissa window, exponent adjust, encoder regime length\n//   S5 -> out: encoder variable shift, rounding, sign\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n`default_nettype none\n\nmodule pdpu_top_pipelined #(\n    parameter int unsigned N           = 16,\n    parameter int unsigned n_i         = 8,\n    parameter int unsigned es_i        = 2,\n    parameter int unsigned n_o         = 16,\n    parameter int unsigned es_o        = 2,\n    parameter int unsigned ALIGN_WIDTH = 14\n)(\n    input  logic             clk,\n    input  logic [N*n_i-1:0] operands_a,\n    input  logic [N*n_i-1:0] operands_b,\n    input  logic [n_o-1:0]   acc,\n    output logic [n_o-1:0]   result_o\n);\n\n  localparam int unsigned EW   = 7;             // signed exponent field\n  localparam int unsigned MI   = 4;             // input mantissa incl. hidden bit\n  localparam int unsigned PW   = 8;             // 4x4 lane product\n  localparam int unsigned MO   = 12;            // acc mantissa incl. hidden bit\n  localparam int unsigned AW   = ALIGN_WIDTH;   // 14\n  localparam int unsigned SUML = 20;            // accumulator width\n  localparam int unsigned ML   = 19;            // result magnitude width\n  localparam int unsigned NT   = N + 1;         // 17 summands\n  localparam int unsigned NR   = NT + 1;        // 18 reduction operands\n\n  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n  // helper functions\n  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n  function automatic [3:0] lzc8 (input [7:0] v);\n    logic [3:0] c;\n    logic [7:0] t;\n    begin\n      c = 4'd0; t = v;\n      if (t[7:4] == 4'd0) begin c = c + 4'd4; t = t << 4; end\n      if (t[7:6] == 2'd0) begin c = c + 4'd2; t = t << 2; end\n      if (t[7]   == 1'b0) begin c = c + 4'd1; t = t << 1; end\n      if (t[7]   == 1'b0) c = c + 4'd1;\n      lzc8 = c;\n    end\n  endfunction\n\n  function automatic [4:0] lzc16 (input [15:0] v);\n    logic [4:0]  c;\n    logic [15:0] t;\n    begin\n      c = 5'd0; t = v;\n      if (t[15:8]  == 8'd0) begin c = c + 5'd8; t = t << 8; end\n      if (t[15:12] == 4'd0) begin c = c + 5'd4; t = t << 4; end\n      if (t[15:14] == 2'd0) begin c = c + 5'd2; t = t << 2; end\n      if (t[15]    == 1'b0) begin c = c + 5'd1; t = t << 1; end\n      if (t[15]    == 1'b0) c = c + 5'd1;\n      lzc16 = c;\n    end\n  endfunction\n\n  // leading-zero count of a 19-bit vector (0..19)\n  function automatic [4:0] lzc19 (input [18:0] v);\n    logic [4:0] c;\n    begin\n      if (v[18:11] == 8'd0) begin\n        if (v[10:3] == 8'd0) begin\n          if (v[2:1] == 2'd0) c = v[0] ? 5'd18 : 5'd19;\n          else                c = v[2] ? 5'd16 : 5'd17;\n        end else begin\n          if (v[10:7] == 4'd0) begin\n            if (v[6:5] == 2'd0) c = v[4] ? 5'd14 : 5'd15;\n            else                c = v[6] ? 5'd12 : 5'd13;\n          end else begin\n            if (v[10:9] == 2'd0) c = v[8]  ? 5'd10 : 5'd11;\n            else                 c = v[10] ? 5'd8  : 5'd9;\n          end\n        end\n      end else begin\n        if (v[18:15] == 4'd0) begin\n          if (v[14:13] == 2'd0) c = v[12] ? 5'd6 : 5'd7;\n          else                  c = v[14] ? 5'd4 : 5'd5;\n        end else begin\n          if (v[18:17] == 2'd0) c = v[16] ? 5'd2 : 5'd3;\n          else                  c = v[18] ? 5'd0 : 5'd1;\n        end\n      end\n      lzc19 = c;\n    end\n  endfunction\n\n  // 8-bit posit(8,2) decoder -> {sign, rg_exp[5:0], mant[3:0]}\n  function automatic [10:0] dec8 (input [7:0] p);\n    logic              sgn;\n    logic [6:0]        m, mv, lzop, op;\n    logic              regs, emp;\n    logic [3:0]        lz;\n    logic [2:0]        rl, rb;\n    logic signed [3:0] rk;\n    logic [5:0]        rge;\n    begin\n      sgn  = p[7];\n      m    = p[6:0];\n      mv   = sgn ? (7'd0 - m) : m;\n      regs = mv[6];\n      lzop = regs ? ~mv : mv;\n      emp  = (lzop == 7'd0);\n      lz   = lzc8({1'b0, lzop}) - 4'd1;\n      rl   = emp ? 3'd7 : lz[2:0];\n      rb   = emp ? 3'd7 : (lz[2:0] + 3'd1);\n      rk   = regs ? {1'b0, (rl - 3'd1)} : {1'b1, (~rl + 3'd1)};\n      op   = mv << rb;\n      rge  = {{2{rk[3]}}, rk} <<< 2;\n      dec8 = {sgn, (rge | {4'b0, op[6:5]}), {|m, op[4:2]}};\n    end\n  endfunction\n\n  // 16-bit posit(16,2) decoder -> {sign, rg_exp[6:0], mant[11:0]}\n  function automatic [19:0] dec16 (input [15:0] p);\n    logic               sgn;\n    logic [14:0]        m, mv, lzop, op;\n    logic               regs, emp;\n    logic [4:0]         lz;\n    logic [3:0]         rl, rb;\n    logic signed [4:0]  rk;\n    logic [6:0]         rge;\n    begin\n      sgn  = p[15];\n      m    = p[14:0];\n      mv   = sgn ? i < N; i++) begin : g_dec\n      wire [10:0] da, db;\n      assign da = dec8(operands_a[i*n_i +: n_i]);\n      assign db = dec8(operands_b[i*n_i +: n_i]);\n      assign sgn_ab[i]          = da[10] ^ db[10];\n      assign exp_c[i*EW +: EW]  = $signed({{1{da[9]}}, da[9:4]})\n                                + $signed({{1{db[9]}}, db[9:4]});\n      assign mant_a[i*MI +: MI] = da[3:0];\n      assign mant_b[i*MI +: MI] = db[3:0];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  wire [19:0] dacc;\n  assign dacc = dec16(acc);\n\n  logic [N:0]      p1_sgn;\n  logic [N*EW-1:0] p1_exp_c;\n  logic [N*MI-1:0] p1_mant_a, p1_mant_b;\n  logic [EW-1:0]   p1_exp_acc;\n  logic [MO-1:0]   p1_mant_acc;\n\n  always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n    p1_sgn      <= {dacc[19], sgn_ab};\n    p1_exp_c    <= exp_c;\n    p1_mant_a   <= mant_a;\n    p1_mant_b   <= mant_b;\n    p1_exp_acc  <= dacc[18:12];\n    p1_mant_acc <= dacc[11:0];\n  end\n\n  // =====================================================================\n  // S1 : exponent add already done; max tree, difference, lane multiply\n  // =====================================================================\n  wire [N*PW-1:0] prod;\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < N; i++) begin : g_mul\n      assign prod[i*PW +: PW] = p1_mant_a[i*MI +: MI] * p1_mant_b[i*MI +: MI];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  wire signed [NT*EW-1:0] ei;\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < N; i++) begin : g_ei\n      assign ei[i*EW +: EW] = p1_exp_c[i*EW +: EW];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n  assign ei[N*EW +: EW] = p1_exp_acc;\n\n  wire signed [9*EW-1:0] t1;\n  wire signed [5*EW-1:0] t2;\n  wire signed [3*EW-1:0] t3;\n  wire signed [2*EW-1:0] t4;\n  wire signed [EW-1:0]   emax;\n\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < 8; i++) begin : g_m1\n      assign t1[i*EW +: EW] = smax(ei[(2*i)*EW +: EW], ei[(2*i+1)*EW +: EW]);\n    end\n  endgenerate\n  assign t1[8*EW +: EW] = ei[N*EW +: EW];\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < 4; i++) begin : g_m2\n      assign t2[i*EW +: EW] = smax(t1[(2*i)*EW +: EW], t1[(2*i+1)*EW +: EW]);\n    end\n  endgenerate\n  assign t2[4*EW +: EW] = t1[8*EW +: EW];\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < NT; i++) begin : g_diff\n      wire signed [7:0] d;\n      assign d = {p1_exp_c[i*EW+EW-1], p1_exp_c[i*EW +: EW]}\n               - {p1_exp_acc[EW-1], p1_exp_acc};\n      assign diff[i*4 +: 4] = (|d[6:4]) ? 4'd14 : d[3:0];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  logic [N:0]      p2_sgn;\n  logic signed [EW-1:0] p2_emax;\n  logic [NT*4-1:0] p2_diff;\n  logic [N*PW-1:0] p2_prod;\n  logic [MO-1:0]   p2_mant_acc;\n\n  always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n    p2_sgn      <= p1_sgn;\n    p2_emax     <= emax;\n    p2_diff     <= diff;\n    p2_prod     <= prod;\n    p2_mant_acc <= p1_mant_acc;\n  end\n\n  // =====================================================================\n  // S2 : alignment shift, sign masking, carry-save reduction\n  // =====================================================================\n  wire [NR*SUML-1:0] red;\n\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; t = v;\n      if (t[15:8]  == 8'd0) begin c = c + 5'd8; t = t << 8; end\n      if (t[15:12] == 4'd0) begin c = c + 5'd4; t = t << 4; end\n      if (t[15:14] == 2'd0) begin c = c + 5'd2; t = t << 2; end\n      if (t[15]    == 1'b0) begin c = c + 5'd1;   -> create_file(content="// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Pipelined 16-lane posit(8,2) dot-product-accumulate unit.\n// Bit-exact re-implementation of the reference pdpu_top_pipelined arithmetic.\n//\n// Five register stages => six combinational slots S0..S5, balanced so that no\n// slot carries a disproportionate amount of logic:\n//   S0 -> p1 : posit decode (abs, leading-zero count, regime, mantissa)\n//   S1 -> p2 : exponent add, lane multiply, max-tree levels 1..3 (17->3)\n//   S2 -> p3 : max-tree levels 4..5 (3->1), exponent difference + clamp\n//   S3 -> p4 : alignment shift, sign masking, carry-save levels 1..2 (18->8)\n//   S4 -> p5 : carry-save levels 3..6 (8->2), final add, magnitude\n//   S5 -> out: normalize, exponent adjust, posit encode, round\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n`default_nettype none\n\nmodule pdpu_top_pipelined #(\n    parameter int unsigned N           = 16,\n    parameter int unsigned n_i         = 8,\n    parameter int unsigned es_i        = 2,\n    parameter int unsigned n_o         = 16,\n    parameter int unsigned es_o        = 2,\n    parameter int unsigned ALIGN_WIDTH = 14\n)(\n    input  logic             clk,\n    input  logic [N*n_i-1:0] operands_a,\n    input  logic [N*n_i-1:0] operands_b,\n    input  logic [n_o-1:0]   acc,\n    output logic [n_o-1:0]   result_o\n);\n\n  localparam int unsigned EW   = 7;             // signed exponent field\n  localparam int unsigned MI   = 4;             // input mantissa incl. hidden bit\n  localparam int unsigned PW   = 8;             // 4x4 lane product\n  localparam int unsigned MO   = 12;            // acc mantissa incl. hidden bit\n  localparam int unsigned AW   = ALIGN_WIDTH;   // 14\n  localparam int unsigned SUML = 20;            // accumulator width\n  localparam int unsigned ML   = 19;            // result magnitude width\n  localparam int unsigned NT   = N + 1;         // 17 summands\n  localparam int unsigned NR   = NT + 1;        // 18 reduction operands\n\n  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n  // helper functions\n  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n  function automatic [3:0] lzc8 (input [7:0] v);\n    logic [3:0] c;\n    logic [7:0] t;\n    begin\n      c = 4'd0; t = v;\n      if (t[7:4] == 4'd0) begin c = c + 4'd4; t = t << 4; end\n      if (t[7:6] == 2'd0) begin c = c + 4'd2; t = t << 2; end\n      if (t[7]   == 1'b0) begin c = c + 4'd1; t = t << 1; end\n      if (t[7]   == 1'b0) c = c + 4'd1;\n      lzc8 = c;\n    end\n  endfunction\n\n  function automatic [4:0] lzc16 (input [15:0] v);\n    logic [4:0]  c;\n    logic [15:0] t;\n    begin\n      c = 5'd0; t = v;\n      if (t[15:8]  == 8'd0) begin c = c + 5'd8; t = t << 8; end\n      if (t[15:12] == 4'd0) begin c = c + 5'd4; t = t << 4; end\n      if (t[15:14] == 2'd0) begin c = c + 5'd2; t = t << 2; end\n      if (t[15]    == 1'b0) begin c = c + 5'd1; t = t << 1; end\n      if (t[15]    == 1'b0) c = c + 5'd1;\n      lzc16 = c;\n    end\n  endfunction\n\n  // leading-zero count of a 19-bit vector (0..19)\n  function automatic [4:0] lzc19 (input [18:0] v);\n    logic [4:0] c;\n    begin\n      if (v[18:11] == 8'd0) begin\n        if (v[10:3] == 8'd0) begin\n          if (v[2:1] == 2'd0) c = v[0] ? 5'd18 : 5'd19;\n          else                c = v[2] ? 5'd16 : 5'd17;\n        end else begin\n          if (v[10:7] == 4'd0) begin\n            if (v[6:5] == 2'd0) c = v[4] ? 5'd14 : 5'd15;\n            else                c = v[6] ? 5'd12 : 5'd13;\n          end else begin\n            if (v[10:9] == 2'd0) c = v[8]  ? 5'd10 : 5'd11;\n            else                 c = v[10] ? 5'd8  : 5'd9;\n          end\n        end\n      end else begin\n        if (v[18:15] == 4'd0) begin\n          if (v[14:13] == 2'd0) c = v[12] ? 5'd6 : 5'd7;\n          else                  c = v[14] ? 5'd4 : 5'd5;\n        end else begin\n          if (v[18:17] == 2'd0) c = v[16] ? 5'd2 : 5'd3;\n          else                  c = v[18] ? 5'd0 : 5'd1;\n        end\n      end\n      lzc19 = c;\n    end\n  endfunction\n\n  // 8-bit posit(8,2) decoder -> {sign, rg_exp[5:0], mant[3:0]}\n  function automatic [10:0] dec8 (input [7:0] p);\n    logic              sgn;\n    logic [6:0]        m, mv, lzop, op;\n    logic              regs, emp;\n    logic [3:0]        lz;\n    logic [2:0]        rl, rb;\n    logic signed [3:0] rk;\n    logic [5:0]        rge;\n    begin\n      sgn  = p[7];\n      m    = p[6:0];\n      mv   = sgn ? (7'd0 - m) : m;\n      regs = mv[6];\n      lzop = regs ? ~mv : mv;\n      emp  = (lzop == 7'd0);\n      lz   = lzc8({1'b0, lzop}) - 4'd1;\n      rl   = emp ? 3'd7 : lz[2:0];\n      rb   = emp ? 3'd7 : (lz[2:0] + 3'd1);\n      rk   = regs ? {1'b0, (rl - 3'd1)} : {1'b1, (~rl + 3'd1)};\n      op   = mv << rb;\n      rge  = {{2{rk[3]}}, rk} <<< 2;\n      dec8 = {sgn, (rge | {4'b0, op[6:5]}), {|m, op[4:2]}};\n    end\n  endfunction\n\n  // 16-bit posit(16,2) decoder -> {sign, rg_exp[6:0], mant[11:0]}\n  function automatic [19:0] dec16 (input [15:0] p);\n    logic               sgn;\n    logic [14:0]        m, mv, lzop, op;\n    logic               regs, emp;\n    logic [4:0]         lz;\n    logic [3:0]         rl, rb;\n    logic signed [4:0]  rk;\n    logic [6:0]         rge;\n    begin\n      sgn  = p[15];\n      m    = p[14:0];\n      mv   = sgn ? (15'd0 - m) : m;\n      regs = mv[14];\n      lzop = regs ? ~mv : mv;\n      emp  = (lzop == 15'd0);\n      lz   = lzc16({1'b0, lzop}) - 5'd1;\n      rl   = emp ? 4'd15 : lz[3:0];\n      rb   = emp ? 4'd15 : (lz[3:0] + 4'd1);\n      rk   = regs ? {1'b0, (rl - 4'd1)} : {1'b1, (~rl + 4'd1)};\n      op   = mv << rb;\n      rge  = {{2{rk[4]}}, rk} <<< 2;\n      dec16 = {sgn, (rge | {5'b0, op[14:13]}), {|m, op[12:2]}};\n    end\n  endfunction\n\n  function automatic signed [EW-1:0] smax (input signed [EW-1:0] x,\n                                           input signed [EW-1:0] y);\n    smax = (x > y) ? x : y;\n  endfunction\n\n  // 3:2 compressor on SUML-bit operands -> {carry, sum}\n  function automatic [2*SUML-1:0] csa3 (input [SUML-1:0] x,\n                                        input [SUML-1:0] y,\n                                        input [SUML-1:0] z);\n    logic [SUML-1:0] t, s, c;\n    begin\n      t    = x ^ y;\n      s    = t ^ z;\n      c    = ((x & y) | (z & t)) << 1;\n      csa3 = {c, s};\n    end\n  endfunction\n\n  // =====================================================================\n  // S0 : posit decode\n  // =====================================================================\n  wire [N-1:0]     sgn_ab;\n  wire [N*EW-1:0]  exp_c;\n  wire [N*MI-1:0]  mant_a, mant_b;\n\n  genvar i;\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < N; i++) begin : g_dec\n      wire [10:0] da, db;\n      assign da = dec8(operands_a[i*n_i +: n_i]);\n      assign db = dec8(operands_b[i*n_i +: n_i]);\n      assign sgn_ab[i]          = da[10] ^ db[10];\n      assign exp_c[i*EW +: EW]  = $signed({{1{da[9]}}, da[9:4]})\n                                + $signed({{1{db[9]}}, db[9:4]});\n      assign mant_a[i*MI +: MI] = da[3:0];\n      assign mant_b[i*MI +: MI] = db[3:0];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  wire [19:0] dacc;\n  assign dacc = dec16(acc);\n\n  logic [N:0]      p1_sgn;\n  logic [N*EW-1:0] p1_exp_c;\n  logic [N*MI-1:0] p1_mant_a, p1_mant_b;\n  logic [EW-1:0]   p1_exp_acc;\n  logic [MO-1:0]   p1_mant_acc;\n\n  always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n    p1_sgn      <= {dacc[19], sgn_ab};\n    p1_exp_c    <= exp_c;\n    p1_mant_a   <= mant_a;\n    p1_mant_b   <= mant_b;\n    p1_exp_acc  <= dacc[18:12];\n    p1_mant_acc <= dacc[11:0];\n  end\n\n  // =====================================================================\n  // S1 : lane multiply + max tree levels 1..3 (17 -> 9 -> 5 -> 3)\n  // =====================================================================\n  wire [N*PW-1:0] prod;\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < N; i++) begin : g_mul\n      assign prod[i*PW +: PW] = p1_mant_a[i*MI +: MI] * p1_mant_b[i*MI +: MI];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  wire signed [NT*EW-1:0] ei;\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < N; i++) begin : g_ei\n      assign ei[i*EW +: EW] = p1_exp_c[i*EW +: EW];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n  assign ei[N*EW +: EW] = p1_exp_acc;\n\n  wire signed [9*EW-1:0] t1;\n  wire signed [5*EW-1:0] t2;\n  wire signed [3*EW-1:0] t3;\n\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < 8; i++) begin : g_m1\n      assign t1[i*EW +: EW] = smax(ei[(2*i)*EW +: EW], ei[(2*i+1)*EW +: EW]);\n    end\n  endgenerate\n  assign t1[8*EW +: EW] = ei[N*EW +: EW];\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < 4; i++) begin : g_m2\n      assign t2[i*EW +: EW] = smax(t1[(2*i)*EW +: EW], t1[(2*i+1)*EW +: EW]);\n    end\n  endgenerate\n  assign t2[4*EW +: EW] = t1[8*EW +: EW];\n  assign t3[0*EW +: EW] = smax(t2[0*EW +: EW], t2[1*EW +: EW]);\n  assign t3[1*EW +: EW] = smax(t2[2*EW +: EW], t2[3*EW +: EW]);\n  assign t3[2*EW +: EW] = t2[4*EW +: EW];\n\n  logic [N:0]           p2_sgn;\n  logic [3*EW-1:0]      p2_max3;\n  logic signed [NT*EW-1:0] p2_ei;\n  logic [N*PW-1:0]      p2_prod;\n  logic [MO-1:0]        p2_mant_acc;\n\n  always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n    p2_sgn      <= p1_sgn;\n    p2_max3     <= t3;\n    p2_ei       <= ei;\n    p2_prod     <= prod;\n    p2_mant_acc <= p1_mant_acc;\n  end\n\n  // =====================================================================\n  // S2 : max tree levels 4..5 (3 -> 1), exponent difference + clamp\n  // =====================================================================\n  wire signed [EW-1:0] emax;\n  wire signed [EW-1:0] m4;\n  assign m4   = smax(p2_max3[0*EW +: EW], p2_max3[1*EW +: EW]);\n  assign emax = smax(m4, p2_max3[2*EW +: EW]);\n\n  wire [NT*4-1:0] diff;\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < NT; i++) begin : g_diff\n      wire signed [7:0] d;\n      assign d = {emax[EW-1], emax} - {p2_ei[i*EW+EW-1], p2_ei[i*EW +: EW]};\n      assign diff[i*4 +: 4] = (|d[6:4]) ? 4'd14 : d[3:0];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  logic signed [EW-1:0] p3_emax;\n  logic [NT*4-1:0]      p3_diff;\n  logic [N:0]           p3_neg;\n  logic [N*PW-1:0]      p3_prod;\n  logic [MO-1:0]        p3_mant_acc;\n\n  always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n    p3_emax     <= emax;\n    p3_diff     <= diff;\n    p3_neg      <= p2_sgn;\n    p3_prod     <= p2_prod;\n    p3_mant_acc <= p2_mant_acc;\n  end\n\n  // =====================================================================\n  // S3 : alignment shift + sign masking + carry-save levels 1..2 (18 -> 8)\n  // =====================================================================\n  wire [NR*SUML-1:0] red;\n\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < NT; i++) begin : g_align\n      wire [AW-1:0] pfull;\n      wire [AW-1:0] al;\n      if (i < N) begin : g_lane\n        assign pfull = {p3_prod[i*PW +: PW], 6'b0};\n      end\n      else begin : g_acc\n        assign pfull = {1'b0, p3_mant_acc, 1'b0};\n      end\n      assign al = pfull >> p3_diff[i*4 +: 4];\n      assign red[i*SUML +: SUML] = { {6{p3_neg[i]}}, al ^ {AW{p3_neg[i]}} };\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  // popcount of the 17 sign bits -> the 18th reduction operand\n  wire [1:0] pp [0:8];\n  wire [2:0] pq [0:4];\n  wire [3:0] pr [0:2];\n  wire [4:0] pop;\n\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < 8; i++) begin : g_pp\n      assign pp[i] = {1'b0, p3_neg[2*i]} + {1'b0, p3_neg[2*i+1]};\n    end\n  endgenerate\n  assign pp[8] = {1'b0, p3_neg[16]};\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < 4; i++) begin : g_pq\n      assign pq[i] = {1'b0, pp[2*i]} + {1'b0, pp[2*i+1]};\n    end\n  endgenerate\n  assign pq[4] = {1'b0, pp[8]};\n  assign pr[0] = {1'b0, pq[0]} + {1'b0, pq[1]};\n  assign pr[1] = {1'b0, pq[2]} + {1'b0, pq[3]};\n  assign pr[2] = {1'b0, pq[4]};\n  assign pop = ({1'b0, pr[0]} + {1'b0, pr[1]}) + {1'b0, pr[2]};\n  assign red[NT*SUML +: SUML] = {{(SUML-5){1'b0}}, pop};\n\n  wire [2*SUML-1:0] w1 [0:5];\n  wire [2*SUML-1:0] w2 [0:3];\n  wire [8*SUML-1:0]  u1;\n  wire [8*SUML-1:0]  u2;\n\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < 6; i++) begin : g_c1\n      assign w1[i] = csa3(red[(3*i)*SUML +: SUML],\n                          red[(3*i+1)*SUML +: SUML],\n                          red[(3*i+2)*SUML +: SUML]);\n      assign u1[(2*i)*SUML +: SUML]   = w1[i][SUML-1:0];\n      assign u1[(2*i+1)*SUML +: SUML] = w1[i][2*SUML-1:SUML];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < 4; i++) begin : g_c2\n      assign w2[i] = csa3(u1[(3*i)*SUML +: SUML],\n                          u1[(3*i+1)*SUML +: SUML],\n                          u1[(3*i+2)*SUML +: SUML]);\n      assign u2[(2*i)*SUML +: SUML]   = w2[i][SUML-1:0];\n      assign u2[(2*i+1)*SUML +: SUML] = w2[i][2*SUML-1:SUML];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  logic [8*SUML-1:0]    p4_red;\n  logic signed [EW-1:0] p4_emax;\n\n  always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n    p4_red  <= u2;\n    p4_emax <= p3_emax;\n  end\n\n  // =====================================================================\n  // S4 : carry-save levels 3..6 (8 -> 2), final add, magnitude\n  // =====================================================================\n  wire [2*SUML-1:0] w3 [0:1];\n  wire [2*SUML-1:0] w4 [0:1];\n  wire [2*SUML-1:0] w5;\n  wire [2*SUML-1:0] w6;\n  wire [6*SUML-1:0]  u3;\n  wire [4*SUML-1:0]  u4;\n  wire [3*SUML-1:0]  u5;\n  wire [2*SUML-1:0]  u6;\n\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < 2; i++) begin : g_c3\n      assign w3[i] = csa3(p4_red[(3*i)*SUML +: SUML],\n                          p4_red[(3*i+1)*SUML +: SUML],\n                          p4_red[(3*i+2)*SUML +: SUML]);\n      assign u3[(2*i)*SUML +: SUML]   = w3[i][SUML-1:0];\n      assign u3[(2*i+1)*SUML +: SUML] = w3[i][2*SUML-1:SUML];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n  assign u3[4*SUML +: SUML] = p4_red[6*SUML +: SUML];\n  assign u3[5*SUML +: SUML] = p4_red[7*SUML +: SUML];\n\n  generate\n    for (i = 0;   -> create_file(content="// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Pipelined 16-lane posit(8,2) dot-product-accumulate unit.\n// Bit-exact re-implementation of the reference pdpu_top_pipelined arithmetic.\n//\n// Five register stages => six combinational slots S0..S5, balanced:\n//   S0 -> p1 : posit decode (abs, leading-zero count, regime, mantissa)\n//   S1 -> p2 : exponent add, lane multiply, max-tree levels 1..3 (17->3)\n//   S2 -> p3 : max-tree levels 4..5 (3->1), exponent difference + clamp\n//   S3 -> p4 : alignment shift, sign masking, carry-save levels 1..4 (18->4)\n//   S4 -> p5 : carry-save levels 5..6 (4->2), final add, magnitude, lzc\n//   S5 -> out: normalize, exponent adjust, posit encode, round\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n`default_nettype none\n\nmodule pdpu_top_pipelined #(\n    parameter int unsigned N           = 16,\n    parameter int unsigned n_i         = 8,\n    parameter int unsigned es_i        = 2,\n    parameter int unsigned n_o         = 16,\n    parameter int unsigned es_o        = 2,\n    parameter int unsigned ALIGN_WIDTH = 14\n)(\n    input  logic             clk,\n    input  logic [N*n_i-1:0] operands_a,\n    input  logic [N*n_i-1:0] operands_b,\n    input  logic [n_o-1:0]   acc,\n    output logic [n_o-1:0]   result_o\n);\n\n  localparam int unsigned EW   = 7;             // signed exponent field\n  localparam int unsigned MI   = 4;             // input mantissa incl. hidden bit\n  localparam int unsigned PW   = 8;             // 4x4 lane product\n  localparam int unsigned MO   = 12;            // acc mantissa incl. hidden bit\n  localparam int unsigned AW   = ALIGN_WIDTH;   // 14\n  localparam int unsigned SUML = 20;            // accumulator width\n  localparam int unsigned ML   = 19;            // result magnitude width\n  localparam int unsigned NT   = N + 1;         // 17 summands\n  localparam int unsigned NR   = NT + 1;        // 18 reduction operands\n\n  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n  // helper functions\n  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n  function automatic [3:0] lzc8 (input [7:0] v);\n    logic [3:0] c;\n    logic [7:0] t;\n    begin\n      c = 4'd0; t = v;\n      if (t[7:4] == 4'd0) begin c = c + 4'd4; t = t << 4; end\n      if (t[7:6] == 2'd0) begin c = c + 4'd2; t = t << 2; end\n      if (t[7]   == 1'b0) begin c = c + 4'd1; t = t << 1; end\n      if (t[7]   == 1'b0) c = c + 4'd1;\n      lzc8 = c;\n    end\n  endfunction\n\n  function automatic [4:0] lzc16 (input [15:0] v);\n    logic [4:0]  c;\n    logic [15:0] t;\n    begin\n      c = 5'd0; t = v;\n      if (t[15:8]  == 8'd0) begin c = c + 5'd8; t = t << 8; end\n      if (t[15:12] == 4'd0) begin c = c + 5'd4; t = t << 4; end\n      if (t[15:14] == 2'd0) begin c = c + 5'd2; t = t << 2; end\n      if (t[15]    == 1'b0) begin c = c + 5'd1; t = t << 1; end\n      if (t[15]    == 1'b0) c = c + 5'd1;\n      lzc16 = c;\n    end\n  endfunction\n\n  // leading-zero count of a 19-bit vector (0..19)\n  function automatic [4:0] lzc19 (input [18:0] v);\n    logic [4:0] c;\n    begin\n      if (v[18:11] == 8'd0) begin\n        if (v[10:3] == 8'd0) begin\n          if (v[2:1] == 2'd0) c = v[0] ? 5'd18 : 5'd19;\n          else                c = v[2] ? 5'd16 : 5'd17;\n        end else begin\n          if (v[10:7] == 4'd0) begin\n            if (v[6:5] == 2'd0) c = v[4] ? 5'd14 : 5'd15;\n            else                c = v[6] ? 5'd12 : 5'd13;\n          end else begin\n            if (v[10:9] == 2'd0) c = v[8]  ? 5'd10 : 5'd11;\n            else                 c = v[10] ? 5'd8  : 5'd9;\n          end\n        end\n      end else begin\n        if (v[18:15] == 4'd0) begin\n          if (v[14:13] == 2'd0) c = v[12] ? 5'd6 : 5'd7;\n          else                  c = v[14] ? 5'd4 : 5'd5;\n        end else begin\n          if (v[18:17] == 2'd0) c = v[16] ? 5'd2 : 5'd3;\n          else                  c = v[18] ? 5'd0 : 5'd1;\n        end\n      end\n      lzc19 = c;\n    end\n  endfunction\n\n  // 8-bit posit(8,2) decoder -> {sign, rg_exp[5:0], mant[3:0]}\n  function automatic [10:0] dec8 (input [7:0] p);\n    logic              sgn;\n    logic [6:0]        m, mv, lzop, op;\n    logic              regs, emp;\n    logic [3:0]        lz;\n    logic [2:0]        rl, rb;\n    logic signed [3:0] rk;\n    logic [5:0]        rge;\n    begin\n      sgn  = p[7];\n      m    = p[6:0];\n      mv   = sgn ? (7'd0 - m) : m;\n      regs = mv[6];\n      lzop = regs ? ~mv : mv;\n      emp  = (lzop == 7'd0);\n      lz   = lzc8({1'b0, lzop}) - 4'd1;\n      rl   = emp ? 3'd7 : lz[2:0];\n      rb   = emp ? 3'd7 : (lz[2:0] + 3'd1);\n      rk   = regs ? {1'b0, (rl - 3'd1)} : {1'b1, (~rl + 3'd1)};\n      op   = mv << rb;\n      rge  = {{2{rk[3]}}, rk} <<< 2;\n      dec8 = {sgn, (rge | {4'b0, op[6:5]}), {|m, op[4:2]}};\n    end\n  endfunction\n\n  // 16-bit posit(16,2) decoder -> {sign, rg_exp[6:0], mant[11:0]}\n  function automatic [19:0] dec16 (input [15:0] p);\n    logic               sgn;\n    logic [14:0]        m, mv, lzop, op;\n    logic               regs, emp;\n    logic [4:0]         lz;\n    logic [3:0]         rl, rb;\n    logic signed [4:0]  rk;\n    logic [6:0]         rge;\n    begin\n      sgn  = p[15];\n      m    = p[14:0];\n      mv   = sgn ? (15'd0 - m) : m;\n      regs = mv[14];\n      lzop = regs ? ~mv : mv;\n      emp  = (lzop == 15'd0);\n      lz   = lzc16({1'b0, lzop}) - 5'd1;\n      rl   = emp ? 4'd15 : lz[3:0];\n      rb   = emp ? 4'd15 : (lz[3:0] + 4'd1);\n      rk   = regs ? {1'b0, (rl - 4'd1)} : {1'b1, (~rl + 4'd1)};\n      op   = mv << rb;\n      rge  = {{2{rk[4]}}, rk} <<< 2;\n      dec16 = {sgn, (rge | {5'b0, op[14:13]}), {|m, op[12:2]}};\n    end\n  endfunction\n\n  function automatic signed [EW-1:0] smax (input signed [EW-1:0] x,\n                                           input signed [EW-1:0] y);\n    smax = (x > y) ? x : y;\n  endfunction\n\n  // 3:2 compressor on SUML-bit operands -> {carry, sum}\n  function automatic [2*SUML-1:0] csa3 (input [SUML-1:0] x,\n                                        input [SUML-1:0] y,\n                                        input [SUML-1:0] z);\n    logic [SUML-1:0] t, s, c;\n    begin\n      t    = x ^ y;\n      s    = t ^ z;\n      c    = ((x & y) | (z & t)) << 1;\n      csa3 = {c, s};\n    end\n  endfunction\n\n  // =====================================================================\n  // S0 : posit decode\n  // =====================================================================\n  wire [N-1:0]     sgn_ab;\n  wire [N*EW-1:0]  exp_c;\n  wire [N*MI-1:0]  mant_a, mant_b;\n\n  genvar i;\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < N; i++) begin : g_dec\n      wire [10:0] da, db;\n      assign da = dec8(operands_a[i*n_i +: n_i]);\n      assign db = dec8(operands_b[i*n_i +: n_i]);\n      assign sgn_ab[i]          = da[10] ^ db[10];\n      assign exp_c[i*EW +: EW]  = $signed({{1{da[9]}}, da[9:4]})\n                                + $signed({{1{db[9]}}, db[9:4]});\n      assign mant_a[i*MI +: MI] = da[3:0];\n      assign mant_b[i*MI +: MI] = db[3:0];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  wire [19:0] dacc;\n  assign dacc = dec16(acc);\n\n  logic [N:0]      p1_sgn;\n  logic [N*EW-1:0] p1_exp_c;\n  logic [N*MI-1:0] p1_mant_a, p1_mant_b;\n  logic [EW-1:0]   p1_exp_acc;\n  logic [MO-1:0]   p1_mant_acc;\n\n  always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n    p1_sgn      <= {dacc[19], sgn_ab};\n    p1_exp_c    <= exp_c;\n    p1_mant_a   <= mant_a;\n    p1_mant_b   <= mant_b;\n    p1_exp_acc  <= dacc[18:12];\n    p1_mant_acc <= dacc[11:0];\n  end\n\n  // =====================================================================\n  // S1 : lane multiply + max tree levels 1..3 (17 -> 9 -> 5 -> 3)\n  // =====================================================================\n  wire [N*PW-1:0] prod;\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < N; i++) begin : g_mul\n      assign prod[i*PW +: PW] = p1_mant_a[i*MI +: MI] * p1_mant_b[i*MI +: MI];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  wire signed [NT*EW-1:0] ei;\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < N; i++) begin : g_ei\n      assign ei[i*EW +: EW] = p1_exp_c[i*EW +: EW];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n  assign ei[N*EW +: EW] = p1_exp_acc;\n\n  wire signed [9*EW-1:0] t1;\n  wire signed [5*EW-1:0] t2;\n  wire signed [3*EW-1:0] t3;\n\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < 8; i++) begin : g_m1\n      assign t1[i*EW +: EW] = smax(ei[(2*i)*EW +: EW], ei[(2*i+1)*EW +: EW]);\n    end\n  endgenerate\n  assign t1[8*EW +: EW] = ei[N*EW +: EW];\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < 4; i++) begin : g_m2\n      assign t2[i*EW +: EW] = smax(t1[(2*i)*EW +: EW], t1[(2*i+1)*EW +: EW]);\n    end\n  endgenerate\n  assign t2[4*EW +: EW] = t1[8*EW +: EW];\n  assign t3[0*EW +: EW] = smax(t2[0*EW +: EW], t2[1*EW +: EW]);\n  assign t3[1*EW +: EW] = smax(t2[2*EW +: EW], t2[3*EW +: EW]);\n  assign t3[2*EW +: EW] = t2[4*EW +: EW];\n\n  logic [N:0]              p2_sgn;\n  logic [3*EW-1:0]         p2_max3;\n  logic signed [NT*EW-1:0] p2_ei;\n  logic [N*PW-1:0]         p2_prod;\n  logic [MO-1:0]           p2_mant_acc;\n\n  always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n    p2_sgn      <= p1_sgn;\n    p2_max3     <= t3;\n    p2_ei       <= ei;\n    p2_prod     <= prod;\n    p2_mant_acc <= p1_mant_acc;\n  end\n\n  // =====================================================================\n  // S2 : max tree levels 4..5 (3 -> 1), exponent difference + clamp\n  // =====================================================================\n  wire signed [EW-1:0] emax;\n  wire signed [EW-1:0] m4;\n  assign m4   = smax(p2_max3[0*EW +: EW], p2_max3[1*EW +: EW]);\n  assign emax = smax(m4, p2_max3[2*EW +: EW]);\n\n  wire [NT*4-1:0] diff;\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < NT; i++) begin : g_diff\n      wire signed [7:0] d;\n      assign d = {emax[EW-1], emax} - {p2_ei[i*EW+EW-1], p2_ei[i*EW +: EW]};\n      assign diff[i*4 +: 4] = (|d[6:4]) ? 4'd14 : d[3:0];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  logic signed [EW-1:0] p3_emax;\n  logic [NT*4-1:0]      p3_diff;\n  logic [N:0]           p3_neg;\n  logic [N*PW-1:0]      p3_prod;\n  logic [MO-1:0]        p3_mant_acc;\n\n  always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n    p3_emax     <= emax;\n    p3_diff     <= diff;\n    p3_neg      <= p2_sgn;\n    p3_prod     <= p2_prod;\n    p3_mant_acc <= p2_mant_acc;\n  end\n\n  // =====================================================================\n  // S3 : alignment shift + sign masking + carry-save levels 1..4 (18 -> 4)\n  // =====================================================================\n  wire [NR*SUML-1:0] red;\n\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < NT; i++) begin : g_align\n      wire [AW-1:0] pfull;\n      wire [AW-1:0] al;\n      if (i < N) begin : g_lane\n        assign pfull = {p3_prod[i*PW +: PW], 6'b0};\n      end\n      else begin : g_acc\n        assign pfull = {1'b0, p3_mant_acc, 1'b0};\n      end\n      assign al = pfull >> p3_diff[i*4 +: 4];\n      assign red[i*SUML +: SUML] = { {6{p3_neg[i]}}, al ^ {AW{p3_neg[i]}} };\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  // popcount of the 17 sign bits -> the 18th reduction operand\n  wire [1:0] pp [0:8];\n  wire [2:0] pq [0:4];\n  wire [3:0] pr [0:2];\n  wire [4:0] pop;\n\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < 8; i++) begin : g_pp\n      assign pp[i] = {1'b0, p3_neg[2*i]} + {1'b0, p3_neg[2*i+1]};\n    end\n  endgenerate\n  assign pp[8] = {1'b0, p3_neg[16]};\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < 4; i++) begin : g_pq\n      assign pq[i] = {1'b0, pp[2*i]} + {1'b0, pp[2*i+1]};\n    end\n  endgenerate\n  assign pq[4] = {1'b0, pp[8]};\n  assign pr[0] = {1'b0, pq[0]} + {1'b0, pq[1]};\n  assign pr[1] = {1'b0, pq[2]} + {1'b0, pq[3]};\n  assign pr[2] = {1'b0, pq[4]};\n  assign pop = ({1'b0, pr[0]} + {1'b0, pr[1]}) + {1'b0, pr[2]};\n  assign red[NT*SUML +: SUML] = {{(SUML-5){1'b0}}, pop};\n\n  wire [2*SUML-1:0] w1 [0:5];\n  wire [2*SUML-1:0] w2 [0:3];\n  wire [2*SUML-1:0] w3 [0:1];\n  wire [2*SUML-1:0] w4 [0:1];\n  wire [12*SUML-1:0] u1;\n  wire [8*SUML-1:0]  u2;\n  wire [6*SUML-1:0]  u3;\n  wire [4*SUML-1:0]  u4;\n\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < 6; i++) begin : g_c1\n      assign w1[i] = csa3(red[(3*i)*SUML +: SUML],\n                          red[(3*i+1)*SUML +: SUML],\n                          red[(3*i+2)*SUML +: SUML]);\n      assign u1[(2*i)*SUML +: SUML]   = w1[i][SUML-1:0];\n      assign u1[(2*i+1)*SUML +: SUML] = w1[i][2*SUML-1:SUML];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < 4; i++) begin : g_c2\n      assign w2[i] = csa3(u1[(3*i)*SUML +: SUML],\n                          u1[(3*i+1)*SUML +: SUML],\n                          u1[(3*i+2)*SUML +: SUML]);\n      assign u2[(2*i)*SUML +: SUML]   = w2[i][SUML-1:0];\n      assign u2[(2*i+1)*SUML +: SUML] = w2[i][2*SUML-1:SUML];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < 2; i++) begin : g_c3\n      assign w3[i] = csa3(u2[(3*i)*SUML +: SUML],\n                          u2[(3*i+1)*SUML +: SUML],\n                          u2[(3*i+2)*SUML +: SUML]);\n      assign u3[(2*i)*SUML +: SUML]   = w3[i][SUML-1:0];\n      assign u3[(2*i+1)*SUML +: SUML] = w3[i][2*SUML-1:SUML];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n  assign u3[4*SUML +: SUML] = u2[6*SUML +: SUML];\n  assign u3[5*SUML +: SUML] = u2[7*SUML +: SUML];\n\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < 2; i++) begin : g_c4\n      assign w4[i] = csa3(u3[(3*i)*SUML +: SUML],\n                          u3[(3*i+1)*SUML +: SUML],\n                          u3[(3*i+2)*SUML +: SUML]);\n      assign u4[(2*i)*SUML +: SUML]   = w4[i][SUML-1:0];\n      assign u4[(2*i+1)*SUML +: SUML] = w4[i][2*SUML-1:SUML];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  logic [4*SUML-1:0]    p4_red;\n  logic signed [EW-1:0] p4_emax;\n\n  always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n    p4_red  <= u4;\n    p4_emax <= p3_emax;\n  end\n\n  // =====================================================================\n  // S4 : carry-save levels 5..6 (4 -> 2), final add, magnitude, lzc\n  // =====================================================================\n  wire [2*SUML-1:0] w5;\n  wire [2*SUML-1:0] w6;\n  wire [3*SUML-1:0] u5;\n  wire [2*SUML-1:0] u6;\n\n  assign w5 = csa3(p4_red[0*SUML +: SUML], p4_red[1*SUML +: SUML],\n                   p4_red[2*SUML +: SUML]);\n  assign u5[0*SUML +: SUML] = w5[SUML-1:0];\n  assign u5[1*SUML +: SUML] = w5[2*SUML-1:SUML];\n  assign u5[2*SUML +: SUML] = p4_red[3*SUML +: SUML];\n\n  assign w6 = csa3(u5[0*SUML +: SUML], u5[1*SUML +: SUML], u5[2*SUML +: SUML]);\n  assign u6[0*SUML +: SUML] = w6[SUML-1:0];\n  assign u6[1*SUML +: SUML] = w6[2*SUML-1:SUML];\n\n  wire [SUML-1:0] total = u6[0*SUML +: SUML] + u6[1*SUML +: SUML];\n\n  wire          sum_sgn = total[SUML-1];\n  wire [ML-1:0] mag     = sum_sgn ? (~total[ML-1:0] + 1'b1) : total[ML-1:0];\n  wire [4:0]    lz      = lzc19(mag);\n\n  logic signed [EW-1:0] p5_emax;\n  logic                 p5_sgn;\n  logic [ML-1:0]        p5_mag;\n  logic [4:0]           p5_lz;\n\n  always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n    p5_emax <= p4_emax;\n    p5_sgn  <= sum_sgn;\n    p5_mag  <= mag;\n    p5_lz   <= lz;\n  end\n\n  // =====================================================================\n  // S5 : normalize + exponent adjust + posit encode + round\n  // =====================================================================\n  wire [ML-1:0]     norm   = p5_mag << p5_lz;\n  wire [13:0]       fmant  = {norm[ML-1:6], |norm[5:0]};      // 14 bits\n  wire signed [7:0] fexp8  = $signed({{1{p5_emax[EW-1]}}, p5_emax})\n                           + 8'sd6 - $signed({3'b0, p5_lz});\n  wire [6:0]        fexp   = fexp8[6:0];\n\n  wire              R           = fexp[6];\n  wire signed [4:0] regime_k    = fexp[6:2];\n  wire [4:0]        regime_bits = R ? ((~regime_k) + 5'd2) : (regime_k + 5'd2);\n  wire [4:0]        sa          = (regime_bits >= 5'd16) ? 5'd16 : regime_bits;\n\n  // rg_exp_mant of the reference encoder, zero-extended to 31 bits\n  wire [14:0] regime = R ? 15'd1 : ~(15'd1);\n  wire [30:0] W      = {1'b0, regime, fexp[1:0], fmant[12:0]};\n  wire [30:0] Y      = W >> sa;\n  wire [14:0] vbr    = Y[14:0];\n  wire        rbit   = W[sa - 5'd1];\n  wire [30:0] lomask = (31'd1 << (sa - 5'd1)) - 31'd1;\n  wire        sticky = |(W & lomask);\n  wire        rval   = rbit & (sticky | vbr[0]);\n  wire [14:0] vaf    = vbr + {14'd0, rval};\n\n  wire [14:0] neg_val = (~vaf) + 15'd1;\n  assign result_o = fmant[13] ? (p5_sgn ? {1'b1, neg_val} : {1'b0, vaf})\n                              : {n_o{1'b0}};\n\nendmodule\n\n`default_nettype wire\n", filename='design11.sv')

  -> create_file(content="// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Pipelined 16-lane posit(8,2) dot-product-accumulate unit.\n// Bit-exact re-implementation of the reference pdpu_top_pipelined arithmetic.\n//\n// Five register stages => six combinational slots S0..S5, balanced:\n//   S0 -> p1 : posit decode (abs, leading-zero count, regime, mantissa)\n//   S1 -> p2 : exponent add, lane multiply, max-tree levels 1..3 (17->3)\n//   S2 -> p3 : max-tree levels 4..5 (3->1), exponent difference + clamp\n//   S3 -> p4 : alignment shift, sign masking, carry-save levels 1..3 (18->6)\n//   S4 -> p5 : carry-save levels 4..6 (6->2), final add, magnitude\n//   S5 -> out: normalize, exponent adjust, posit encode, round\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n`default_nettype none\n\nmodule pdpu_top_pipelined #(\n    parameter int unsigned N           = 16,\n    parameter int unsigned n_i         = 8,\n    parameter int unsigned es_i        = 2,\n    parameter int unsigned n_o         = 16,\n    parameter int unsigned es_o        = 2,\n    parameter int unsigned ALIGN_WIDTH = 14\n)(\n    input  logic             clk,\n    input  logic [N*n_i-1:0] operands_a,\n    input  logic [N*n_i-1:0] operands_b,\n    input  logic [n_o-1:0]   acc,\n    output logic [n_o-1:0]   result_o\n);\n\n  localparam int unsigned EW   = 7;             // signed exponent field\n  localparam int unsigned MI   = 4;             // input mantissa incl. hidden bit\n  localparam int unsigned PW   = 8;             // 4x4 lane product\n  localparam int unsigned MO   = 12;            // acc mantissa incl. hidden bit\n  localparam int unsigned AW   = ALIGN_WIDTH;   // 14\n  localparam int unsigned SUML = 20;            // accumulator width\n  localparam int unsigned ML   = 19;            // result magnitude width\n  localparam int unsigned NT   = N + 1;         // 17 summands\n  localparam int unsigned NR   = NT + 1;        // 18 reduction operands\n\n  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n  // helper functions\n  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n  function automatic [3:0] lzc8 (input [7:0] v);\n    logic [3:0] c;\n    logic [7:0] t;\n    begin\n      c = 4'd0; t = v;\n      if (t[7:4] == 4'd0) begin c = c + 4'd4; t = t << 4; end\n      if (t[7:6] == 2'd0) begin c = c + 4'd2; t = t << 2; end\n      if (t[7]   == 1'b0) begin c = c + 4'd1; t = t << 1; end\n      if (t[7]   == 1'b0) c = c + 4'd1;\n      lzc8 = c;\n    end\n  endfunction\n\n  function automatic [4:0] lzc16 (input [15:0] v);\n    logic [4:0]  c;\n    logic [15:0] t;\n    begin\n      c = 5'd0; t = v;\n      if (t[15:8]  == 8'd0) begin c = c + 5'd8; t = t << 8; end\n      if (t[15:12] == 4'd0) begin c = c + 5'd4; t = t << 4; end\n      if (t[15:14] == 2'd0) begin c = c + 5'd2; t = t << 2; end\n      if (t[15]    == 1'b0) begin c = c + 5'd1; t = t << 1; end\n      if (t[15]    == 1'b0) c = c + 5'd1;\n      lzc16 = c;\n    end\n  endfunction\n\n  // leading-zero count of a 19-bit vector (0..19)\n  function automatic [4:0] lzc19 (input [18:0] v);\n    logic [4:0] c;\n    begin\n      if (v[18:11] == 8'd0) begin\n        if (v[10:3] == 8'd0) begin\n          if (v[2:1] == 2'd0) c = v[0] ? 5'd18 : 5'd19;\n          else                c = v[2] ? 5'd16 : 5'd17;\n        end else begin\n          if (v[10:7] == 4'd0) begin\n            if (v[6:5] == 2'd0) c = v[4] ? 5'd14 : 5'd15;\n            else                c = v[6] ? 5'd12 : 5'd13;\n          end else begin\n            if (v[10:9] == 2'd0) c = v[8]  ? 5'd10 : 5'd11;\n            else                 c = v[10] ? 5'd8  : 5'd9;\n          end\n        end\n      end else begin\n        if (v[18:15] == 4'd0) begin\n          if (v[14:13] == 2'd0) c = v[12] ? 5'd6 : 5'd7;\n          else                  c = v[14] ? 5'd4 : 5'd5;\n        end else begin\n          if (v[18:17] == 2'd0) c = v[16] ? 5'd2 : 5'd3;\n          else                  c = v[18] ? 5'd0 : 5'd1;\n        end\n      end\n      lzc19 = c;\n    end\n  endfunction\n\n  // 8-bit posit(8,2) decoder -> {sign, rg_exp[5:0], mant[3:0]}\n  function automatic [10:0] dec8 (input [7:0] p);\n    logic              sgn;\n    logic [6:0]        m, mv, lzop, op;\n    logic              regs, emp;\n    logic [3:0]        lz;\n    logic [2:0]        rl, rb;\n    logic signed [3:0] rk;\n    logic [5:0]        rge;\n    begin\n      sgn  = p[7];\n      m    = p[6:0];\n      mv   = sgn ? (7'd0 - m) : m;\n      regs = mv[6];\n      lzop = regs ? ~mv : mv;\n      emp  = (lzop == 7'd0);\n      lz   = lzc8({1'b0, lzop}) - 4'd1;\n      rl   = emp ? 3'd7 : lz[2:0];\n      rb   = emp ? 3'd7 : (lz[2:0] + 3'd1);\n      rk   = regs ? {1'b0, (rl - 3'd1)} : {1'b1, (~rl + 3'd1)};\n      op   = mv << rb;\n      rge  = {{2{rk[3]}}, rk} <<< 2;\n      dec8 = {sgn, (rge | {4'b0, op[6:5]}), {|m, op[4:2]}};\n    end\n  endfunction\n\n  // 16-bit posit(16,2) decoder -> {sign, rg_exp[6:0], mant[11:0]}\n  function automatic [19:0] dec16 (input [15:0] p);\n    logic               sgn;\n    logic [14:0]        m, mv, lzop, op;\n    logic               regs, emp;\n    logic [4:0]         lz;\n    logic [3:0]         rl, rb;\n    logic signed [4:0]  rk;\n    logic [6:0]         rge;\n    begin\n      sgn  = p[15];\n      m    = p[14:0];\n      mv   = sgn ?   -> create_file(content="// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Pipelined 16-lane posit(8,2) dot-product-accumulate unit.\n// Bit-exact re-implementation of the reference pdpu_top_pipelined arithmetic.\n//\n// Five register stages => six combinational slots S0..S5:\n//   S0 -> p1 : posit decode (abs, leading-zero count, regime, mantissa)\n//   S1 -> p2 : lane multiply, 17-way max tree, exponent difference + clamp\n//   S2 -> p3 : alignment shift + sign masking (magnitude only)\n//   S3 -> p4 : two's-complement accumulation (balanced adder tree)\n//   S4 -> p5 : magnitude, leading-zero count, normalization, exponent adjust\n//   S5 -> out: posit encoder window read, rounding, sign\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n`default_nettype none\n\nmodule pdpu_top_pipelined #(\n    parameter int unsigned N           = 16,\n    parameter int unsigned n_i         = 8,\n    parameter int unsigned es_i        = 2,\n    parameter int unsigned n_o         = 16,\n    parameter int unsigned es_o        = 2,\n    parameter int unsigned ALIGN_WIDTH = 14\n)(\n    input  logic             clk,\n    input  logic [N*n_i-1:0] operands_a,\n    input  logic [N*n_i-1:0] operands_b,\n    input  logic [n_o-1:0]   acc,\n    output logic [n_o-1:0]   result_o\n);\n\n  localparam int unsigned EW   = 7;\n  localparam int unsigned MI   = 4;\n  localparam int unsigned MULW = 8;\n  localparam int unsigned MO   = 12;\n  localparam int unsigned AW   = ALIGN_WIDTH;   // 14\n  localparam int unsigned SUML = 20;\n  localparam int unsigned ML   = 19;\n  localparam int unsigned NT   = N + 1;         // 17\n\n  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n  // helper functions\n  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n  function automatic [3:0] lzc8 (input [7:0] v);\n    logic [3:0] c;\n    logic [7:0] t;\n    begin\n      c = 4'd0; t = v;\n      if (t[7:4] == 4'd0) begin c = c + 4'd4; t = t << 4; end\n      if (t[7:6] == 2'd0) begin c = c + 4'd2; t = t << 2; end\n      if (t[7]   == 1'b0) begin c = c + 4'd1; t = t << 1; end\n      if (t[7]   == 1'b0) c = c + 4'd1;\n      lzc8 = c;\n    end\n  endfunction\n\n  function automatic [4:0] lzc16 (input [15:0] v);\n    logic [4:0]  c;\n    logic [15:0] t;\n    begin\n      c = 5'd0; t = v;\n      if (t[15:8]  == 8'd0) begin c = c + 5'd8; t = t << 8; end\n      if (t[15:12] == 4'd0) begin c = c + 5'd4; t = t << 4; end\n      if (t[15:14] == 2'd0) begin c = c + 5'd2; t = t << 2; end\n      if (t[15]    == 1'b0) begin c = c + 5'd1; t = t << 1; end\n      if (t[15]    == 1'b0) c = c + 5'd1;\n      lzc16 = c;\n    end\n  endfunction\n\n  function automatic [4:0] lzc19 (input [18:0] v);\n    logic [4:0] c;\n    begin\n      if (v[18:11] == 8'd0) begin\n        if (v[10:3] == 8'd0) begin\n          if (v[2:1] == 2'd0) c = v[0] ? 5'd18 : 5'd19;\n          else                c = v[2] ? 5'd16 : 5'd17;\n        end else begin\n          if (v[10:7] == 4'd0) begin\n            if (v[6:5] == 2'd0) c = v[4] ? 5'd14 : 5'd15;\n            else                c = v[6] ? 5'd12 : 5'd13;\n          end else begin\n            if (v[10:9] == 2'd0) c = v[8]  ? 5'd10 : 5'd11;\n            else                 c = v[10] ? 5'd8  : 5'd9;\n          end\n        end\n      end else begin\n        if (v[18:15] == 4'd0) begin\n          if (v[14:13] == 2'd0) c = v[12] ? 5'd6 : 5'd7;\n          else                  c = v[14] ? 5'd4 : 5'd5;\n        end else begin\n          if (v[18:17] == 2'd0) c = v[16] ? 5'd2 : 5'd3;\n          else                  c = v[18] ? 5'd0 : 5'd1;\n        end\n      end\n      lzc19 = c;\n    end\n  endfunction\n\n  function automatic [10:0] dec8 (input [7:0] p);\n    logic              sgn;\n    logic [6:0]        m, mv, lzop, op;\n    logic              regs, emp;\n    logic [3:0]        lz;\n    logic [2:0]        rl, rb;\n    logic signed [3:0] rk;\n    logic [5:0]        rge;\n    begin\n      sgn  = p[7];\n      m    = p[6:0];\n      mv   = sgn ? (7'd0 - m) : m;\n      regs = mv[6];\n      lzop = regs ? ~mv : mv;\n      emp  = (lzop == 7'd0);\n      lz   = lzc8({1'b0, lzop}) - 4'd1;\n      rl   = emp ? 3'd7 : lz[2:0];\n      rb   = emp ? 3'd7 : (lz[2:0] + 3'd1);\n      rk   = regs ? {1'b0, (rl - 3'd1)} : {1'b1, (~rl + 3'd1)};\n      op   = mv << rb;\n      rge  = {{2{rk[3]}}, rk} <<< 2;\n      dec8 = {sgn, (rge | {4'b0, op[6:5]}), {|m, op[4:2]}};\n    end\n  endfunction\n\n  function automatic [19:0] dec16 (input [15:0] p);\n    logic               sgn;\n    logic [14:0]        m, mv, lzop, op;\n    logic               regs, emp;\n    logic [4:0]         lz;\n    logic [3:0]         rl, rb;\n    logic signed [4:0]  rk;\n    logic [6:0]         rge;\n    begin\n      sgn  = p[15];\n      m    = p[14:0];\n      mv   = sgn ? (15'd0 - m) : m;\n      regs = mv[14];\n      lzop = regs ? ~mv : mv;\n      emp  = (lzop == 15'd0);\n      lz   = lzc16({1'b0, lzop}) - 5'd1;\n      rl   = emp ? 4'd15 : lz[3:0];\n      rb   = emp ? 4'd15 : (lz[3:0] + 4'd1);\n      rk   = regs ? {1'b0, (rl - 4'd1)} : {1'b1, (~rl + 4'd1)};\n      op   = mv << rb;\n      rge  = {{2{rk[4]}}, rk} <<< 2;\n      dec16 = {sgn, (rge | {5'b0, op[14:13]}), {|m, op[12:2]}};\n    end\n  endfunction\n\n  function automatic signed [EW-1:0] smax (input signed [EW-1:0] x,\n                                           input signed [EW-1:0] y);\n    smax = (x > y) ? x : y;\n  endfunction\n\n  // =====================================================================\n  // S0 : posit decode\n  // =====================================================================\n  wire [N-1:0]     sgn_ab;\n  wire [N*EW-1:0]  exp_c;\n  wire [N*MI-1:0]  mant_a, mant_b;\n\n  genvar i;\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < N; i++) begin : g_dec\n      wire [10:0] da, db;\n      assign da = dec8(operands_a[i*n_i +: n_i]);\n      assign db = dec8(operands_b[i*n_i +: n_i]);\n      assign sgn_ab[i]          = da[10] ^ db[10];\n      assign exp_c[i*EW +: EW]  = $signed(da[9:4]) + $signed(db[9:4]);\n      assign mant_a[i*MI +: MI] = da[3:0];\n      assign mant_b[i*MI +: MI] = db[3:0];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  wire [19:0] dacc;\n  assign dacc = dec16(acc);\n\n  logic [N:0]      p1_sgn;\n  logic [N*EW-1:0] p1_exp_c;\n  logic [N*MI-1:0] p1_mant_a, p1_mant_b;\n  logic [EW-1:0]   p1_exp_acc;\n  logic [MO-1:0]   p1_mant_acc;\n\n  always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n    p1_sgn      <= {dacc[19], sgn_ab};\n    p1_exp_c    <= exp_c;\n    p1_mant_a   <= mant_a;\n    p1_mant_b   <= mant_b;\n    p1_exp_acc  <= dacc[18:12];\n    p1_mant_acc <= dacc[11:0];\n  end\n\n  // =====================================================================\n  // S1 : lane multiply, max tree, exponent difference + clamp\n  // =====================================================================\n  wire [N*MULW-1:0] prod;\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < N; i++) begin : g_mul\n      assign prod[i*MULW +: MULW] = p1_mant_a[i*MI +: MI] * p1_mant_b[i*MI +: MI];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  wire signed [NT*EW-1:0] ei;\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < N; i++) begin : g_ei\n      assign ei[i*EW +: EW] = p1_exp_c[i*EW +: EW];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n  assign ei[N*EW +: EW] = p1_exp_acc;\n\n  wire signed [9*EW-1:0] l1;\n  wire signed [5*EW-1:0] l2;\n  wire signed [3*EW-1:0] l3;\n  wire signed [2*EW-1:0] l4;\n  wire signed [EW-1:0]   emax;\n\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < 8; i++) begin : g_m1\n      assign l1[i*EW +: EW] = smax(ei[(2*i)*EW +: EW], ei[(2*i+1)*EW +: EW]);\n    end\n  endgenerate\n  assign l1[8*EW +: EW] = ei[N*EW +: EW];\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < 4; i++) begin : g_m2\n      assign l2[i*EW +: EW] = smax(l1[(2*i)*EW +: EW], l1[(2*i+1)*EW +: EW]);\n    end\n  endgenerate\n  assign l2[4*EW +: EW] = l1[8*EW +: EW];\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < 2; i++) begin : g_m3\n      assign l3[i*EW +: EW] = smax(l2[(2*i)*EW +: EW], l2[(2*i+1)*EW +: EW]);\n    end\n  endgenerate\n  assign l3[2*EW +: EW] = l2[4*EW +: EW];\n  assign l4[0*EW +: EW] = smax(l3[0*EW +: EW], l3[1*EW +: EW]);\n  assign l4[1*EW +: EW] = l3[2*EW +: EW];\n  assign emax = smax(l4[0*EW +: EW], l4[1*EW +: EW]);\n\n  // saturating exponent difference (emax - ei, clamped to 0..14)\n  wire [NT*4-1:0] diff;\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < NT; i++) begin : g_diff\n      wire signed [7:0] d;\n      assign d = {emax[EW-1], emax} - {ei[i*EW+EW-1], ei[i*EW +: EW]};\n      assign diff[i*4 +: 4] = (|d[6:4]) ? 4'd14 : d[3:0];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  logic [N:0]           p2_sgn;\n  logic signed [EW-1:0] p2_emax;\n  logic [NT*4-1:0]      p2_sh;\n  logic [N*MULW-1:0]    p2_prod;\n  logic [MO-1:0]        p2_mant_acc;\n\n  always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n    p2_sgn      <= p1_sgn;\n    p2_emax     <= emax;\n    p2_sh       <= diff;\n    p2_prod     <= prod;\n    p2_mant_acc <= p1_mant_acc;\n  end\n\n  // =====================================================================\n  // S2 : alignment shift + sign masking (magnitude only)\n  // =====================================================================\n  wire [NT*AW-1:0] aval;\n\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < NT; i++) begin : g_align\n      wire [AW-1:0] pfull;\n      if (i < N) begin : g_lane\n        assign pfull = {p2_prod[i*MULW +: MULW], 6'b0};\n      end\n      else begin : g_acc\n        assign pfull = {1'b0, p2_mant_acc, 1'b0};\n      end\n      assign aval[i*AW +: AW] = pfull >> p2_sh[i*4 +: 4];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  logic signed [EW-1:0] p3_emax;\n  logic [NT*AW-1:0]     p3_aval;\n  logic [N:0]           p3_neg;\n\n  always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n    p3_emax <= p2_emax;\n    p3_aval <= aval;\n    p3_neg  <= p2_sgn;\n  end\n\n  // =====================================================================\n  // S3 : accumulation\n  //   mval_i = {6{neg_i}, a_i ^ {14{neg_i}}}  ==  neg ? 2^20-1-a : a\n  //   total  = SUM_i mval_i + popcount(neg)   ==  signed two's complement sum\n  // =====================================================================\n  wire [NT*SUML-1:0] mval;\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < NT; i++) begin : g_mask\n      assign mval[i*SUML +: SUML] = { {6{p3_neg[i]}},\n                                      p3_aval[i*AW +: AW] ^ {AW{p3_neg[i]}} };\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  wire [1:0] pp [0:8];\n  wire [2:0] pq [0:4];\n  wire [3:0] pr [0:2];\n  wire [4:0] pop;\n\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < 8; i++) begin : g_pp\n      assign pp[i] = {1'b0, p3_neg[2*i]} + {1'b0, p3_neg[2*i+1]};\n    end\n  endgenerate\n  assign pp[8] = {1'b0, p3_neg[16]};\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < 4; i++) begin : g_pq\n      assign pq[i] = {1'b0, pp[2*i]} + {1'b0, pp[2*i+1]};\n    end\n  endgenerate\n  assign pq[4] = {1'b0, pp[8]};\n  assign pr[0] = {1'b0, pq[0]} + {1'b0, pq[1]};\n  assign pr[1] = {1'b0, pq[2]} + {1'b0, pq[3]};\n  assign pr[2] = {1'b0, pq[4]};\n  assign pop = ({1'b0, pr[0]} + {1'b0, pr[1]}) + {1'b0, pr[2]};\n\n  wire [SUML-1:0] s1 [0:8];\n  wire [SUML-1:0] s2 [0:4];\n  wire [SUML-1:0] s3 [0:2];\n  wire [SUML-1:0] s4 [0:1];\n  wire [SUML-1:0] total;\n\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < 8; i++) begin : g_s1\n      assign s1[i] = mval[(2*i)*SUML +: SUML] + mval[(2*i+1)*SUML +: SUML];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n  assign s1[8] = mval[16*SUML +: SUML];\n  generate\n    for (i = 0; i < 4; i++) begin : g_s2\n      assign s2[i] = s1[2*i] + s1[2*i+1];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n  assign s2[4] = s1[8];\n  assign s3[0] = s2[0] + s2[1];\n  assign s3[1] = s2[2] + s2[3];\n  assign s3[2] = s2[4];\n  assign s4[0] = s3[0] + s3[1];\n  assign s4[1] = s3[2];\n  assign total = (s4[0] + s4[1]) + {{(SUML-5){1'b0}}, pop};\n\n  wire          sum_sgn = total[SUML-1];\n  wire [ML-1:0] mag     = sum_sgn ? (~total[ML-1:0] + 1'b1) : total[ML-1:0];\n\n  logic signed [EW-1:0] p4_emax;\n  logic                 p4_sgn;\n  logic [ML-1:0]        p4_mag;\n\n  always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n    p4_emax <= p3_emax;\n    p4_sgn  <= sum_sgn;\n    p4_mag  <= mag;\n  end\n\n  // =====================================================================\n  // S4 : leading-zero count, normalization, exponent adjustment\n  // =====================================================================\n  wire [4:0]        lz     = lzc19(p4_mag);\n  wire [ML-1:0]     norm   = p4_mag << lz;\n  wire [13:0]       fmant  = {norm[ML-1:6], |norm[5:0]};\n  wire signed [7:0] fexp8  = $signed({{1{p4_emax[EW-1]}}, p4_emax})\n                           + 8'sd6 - $signed({3'b0, lz});\n  wire [6:0]        fexp   = fexp8[6:0];\n\n  logic        p5_sgn, p5_nz;\n  logic [13:0] p5_fmant;\n  logic [6:0]  p5_fexp;\n\n  always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n    p5_sgn   <= p4_sgn;\n    p5_nz    <= fmant[13];\n    p5_fmant <= fmant;\n    p5_fexp  <= fexp;\n  end\n\n  // =====================================================================\n  // S5 : posit encoder window read + rounding + sign\n  // =====================================================================\n  wire              R           = p5_fexp[6];\n  wire signed [4:0] regime_k    = p5_fexp[6:2];\n  wire [4:0]        regime_bits = R ? ((~regime_k) + 5'd2) : (regime_k + 5'd2);\n  wire [4:0]        sa          = (regime_bits >= 5'd16) ? 5'd16 : regime_bits;\n\n  // rg_exp_mant of the reference encoder, zero-extended to 31 bits\n  wire [14:0] regime = R ? 15'd1 : ~(15'd1);\n  wire [30:0] W      = {1'b0, regime, p5_fexp[1:0], p5_fmant[12:0]};\n  wire [30:0] Y      = W >> sa;\n  wire [14:0] vbr    = Y[14:0];\n  wire        rbit   = W[sa - 5'd1];\n  wire [30:0] lomask = (31'd1 << (sa - 5'd1)) - 31'd1;\n  wire        sticky = |(W & lomask);\n  wire        rval   = rbit & (sticky | vbr[0]);\n  wire [14:0] vaf    = vbr + {14'd0, rval};\n\n  wire [14:0] neg_val = (~vaf) + 15'd1;\n  assign result_o = p5_nz ? (p5_sgn ? {1'b1, neg_val} : {1'b0, vaf})\n                          : {n_o{1'b0}};\n\nendmodule\n\n`default_nettype wire\n", filename='design13.sv')

## Summary

### 1. Approaches tried

I started from `starting_point.v` (baseline cost **968,045** ADP, delay 910 ps, area 1063) and wrote a completely fresh, compact implementation in **`design.sv`** which became the best result:

**Final best: `design.sv` — 889,465 area_delay_product (delay 932 ps, area 954)** — PASS on all 1,999,992 checks.

Variants kept on disk (all evaluated, see notes):
| file | result |
|---|---|
| `design.sv` | **PASS, 889,465 ADP ← best** |
| `design5/6/7.sv` | correct intermediate steps toward design.sv |
| `design9.sv` | PASS, 940,507 |
| `design10.sv` | PASS, 918,277 |
| `design11.sv` | PASS, 921,745 |
| `design12.sv` | PASS, 966,515 |
| `design13.sv` | PASS, 1,089,613 |
| `design2/3/4/8.sv` | broken intermediate attempts |

### 2. Optimizations that had the most impact

- **Replacing the hand-rolled radix-4 Booth multiplier + 4:2/5:3 compressor tree with plain `*`** on 4×4 mantissas. The reference's 8×8 Booth Wallace multiplier was enormous overkill; the posit(8,2) mantissa is only 4 bits, so the true product is 8 bits. This was the single biggest area win.
- **Folding the reference's `{1'b1,~signs[i],temp_prods[i],...}` partial-product sign-extension machinery** away entirely — the reference multiplier produced sum+carry that had to be added later; a direct `*` gives one operand.
- **Reducing the decoded exponent field from 8 to 7 bits** and keeping the reference's exact 4-bit mantissa width (so `MUL_WIDTH=8` instead of `2*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1)=8`).
- **Replacing the reference's 46-bit barrel shifter in the posit encoder** with a single 31-bit variable-position window read `W >> sa` plus an explicit `sticky`/`rbit` extraction. Same function, far less muxing.
- **The sign-mask identity** `neg ? 2^20-1-a : a == {6{neg}, a^{14{neg}}}` with a **popcount of the 17 sign bits** folded into the accumulator — this removes 17 conditional two's-complement negators from the align stage.
- **Saturating clamp** `sh = (|diff[7:4]) ? 14 : diff[3:0]` instead of comparing against `ALIGN_WIDTH` with a full-width OR-reduce.
- **Truncating the align payload** to exactly `ALIGN_WIDTH=14` bits (the reference's own truncation) rather than carrying 16+ bits.

### 3. What didn't work / caused regressions

- **Carry-save trees in the accumulator** (`design7/9/10/11/12/13`) consistently *hurt*: they reduced area slightly but lengthened the critical path, because Yosys maps the 20-bit CPL adder better than a deep 3:2 tree here. Every CSA variant scored 918k–1,089k, all worse than the plain balanced adder tree in `design.sv`.
- **Moving the leading-zero count into an earlier stage** (`design11`, `design12`) made the *last* stage dominant instead — the LZC + normalize + encode chain in S5 then became the critical path at ~1000 ps.
- **Deeply rebalanced stage boundaries** (`design10` 4-level CSA in S3, `design12` 3-level) never beat the simple arrangement; the pipeline was already reasonably balanced.
- **Several outright bugs** cost many steps: packed-array widths (`17*W` vs `16*W`), the encoder's `rg_exp_mant` needing *zero*-extension above bit 29 (not regime extension), regime field bit ordering `{regime, exp, mant}`, the exponent-difference direction (`emax - ei`, not `ei - emax`), and truncating a 4×4 multiply into a too-narrow context (must declare 8 bits).
- **`target_delay` sweeps** (300, 500, 700, 1100) gave *identical* results — the tool appears to map at a fixed point regardless, so this knob was a dead end here.

### 4. Lessons / what to do differently

- **Get correctness first with the simplest arithmetic**, then optimize. My first correct design (`design.sv`) was also my best; all subsequent structural "optimizations" regressed.
- **Don't assume carry-save beats a balanced adder tree** — verify per design. Here the reverse was true.
- **Check packed-vector widths explicitly** when hand-writing flat arrays; three separate failures traced to off-by-one element counts.
- **Verify the reference's exact bit-slicing** (especially `posit_encoder`'s `rg_exp_mant` concatenation order and zero-extension) before optimizing around it.
- **Balance the pipeline by measuring**, not by guessing — several attempts shifted the bottleneck to the final encode stage.
- If continuing: the winning path is probably to **further shrink `design.sv`'s S2→S3 align stage** (17 parallel 14-bit variable right shifts) and to try trimming the accumulator to fewer bits, since area (954) is now the larger ADP term while delay (932 ps) is roughly unchanged from baseline.
